vidhe prītyā mama idam vacanam śṛṇu kailāsa vāsinam rudram mad rūpam pūrṇam uttamam deva kārya artham mud bhūtam pṛthak ākṛti dhāriṇam etad arthe hi mad rūpaḥ paripūrṇatamaḥ prabhuḥ saha iti śeṣaḥ cara acarāṇām sarveṣām sura ādīnām ca sarvadā iha api prāpnuyāt kaṣṭam putra pautra vivarjitaḥ rādhayā sahitaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sva sthānam sa gaṇaḥ yayau muhur muhur praṇamya īśam vaikuṃṭha yayatuḥ hṛtam tān ādāya brahma viṣṇu kailāsam yayatuḥ girim tatra dṛṣṭvā maheśānam pārvatī vallabham prabhum dīna rakṣā ātta deham ca sa guṇam deva nāyakam tuṣṭuvuḥ pūrvavat sarve bhaktyā gadgadayā girā devāḥ ūcuḥ rakṣa devān bhaya ākulān śaṃkhacūḍa dānava indram jahi deva niṣūdanam tena vikāvitāḥ devāḥ saṃgrāme ca parājitāḥ hṛta adhikārāḥ ku tale vicaranti yathā narāḥ deva lokaḥ hi durhaśyaḥ teṣām āsīt ca tad bhayāt dīna uddhara kṛpā sindho devān uddhara saṃkaṭāt śakram bhayāt mahā īśāna hatvā tam dānava adhipam śrī śaṃkaraḥ uvāca śaṃkhacūḍam vadhiṣyāmi sa gaṇam na atra saṃśayaḥ iti ākarṇya maheśasya vacaḥ pīyūṣa saṃnibham te sarve pramudā hi āsan naṣṭam matvā ca dānavam praṇipatya maheśam ca sura ādyāḥ sva padam yayuḥ iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadhe śivopadeśaḥ nāma ekatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ atha īśānaḥ mahā rudraḥ duṣṭa kālaḥ satām gatiḥ śaṃkhacūḍa vadham citte niścikāya sura icchayā atikramya ca tad dāram jagāma abhyaṃtare mudā atīva sundaram ramyam samalaṃkṛtam śata koṭi bhiḥ anyaiḥ śva bhramadbhiḥ śastra pāṇibhiḥ puṣpadaṃtaḥ uvāca yat uktam śaṃkareṇa eva tat śṛṇu tvam bravīmi te śivaḥ uvāca rā no ced kuru raṇam sārddham pareṇa ca mayā satām aham kruddhaḥ mahā rudraḥ tvām vadhiṣyāmi asaṃśayam khala daṃḍa dharaḥ aham vai śaraṇa āgata vatsalaḥ rājyam dāsyasi kim vā tvam kariṣyasi raṇam ca kim puṣpadaṃtaḥ uvāca vitatham śaṃbhra vākyam na kadāpi danuja adhipa ittham ca puṣpadaṃtasya śiva dūtasya sat pateḥ ākarṇya vacanam rājā hasitvā tam u vāca saḥ śaṃkhacūḍaḥ uvāca rājyam dāsye na devebhyaḥ vīra bhogyā vasuṃdharā raṇam dāsyāmi te rudra devānām pakṣapātine atas pūrvam aham rudra tvām gamiṣyāmi asaṃśayam prabhāte āgamiṣyāmi vīra yātrā vicārataḥ tvam gaccha ā cakṣva rudrāya hi īdṛśam vacanam mama iti śrutvā śaṃkhacūḍa vacanam su prahasya saḥ rāja indra gaṇānām śaṃkarasya ca na sthātum saṃmukhe yogyaḥ kim punar tasya saṃmukham śaṃkaraḥ paramātmā hi sarveṣām īśvara īśvaraḥ indra ādyāḥ ssakalāḥ devāḥ yasya ājñā vartinaḥ sadā sa prajāpatayaḥ siddhāḥ munayaḥ ca api ahi īśvarāḥ śivasya pūrṇa rūpaḥ ca loka saṃhāra kārakaḥ satām gatiḥ duṣṭa haṃtā nirvikāraḥ parātparaḥ avamānyāḥ na vai tasya śāsanam dānava ṛṣabha kim bahu uktena rāja indra manasā saṃvicārya ca rudram viddhi mahā eṃ śānam param brahma cit ātmakam evam te kuśalam tāta bhaviṣyati anyathā bhayam uvāca śiva dūtam tam bhavitavya vimohitaḥ śaṃkhacūḍaḥ uvāca svatas rājyam na dāsyāmi na adhikārān viniśvayāt iti uktvā śiva dūtaḥ asau jagāma svāminam nijam yathārtha kathayāmāsa puṣpadaṃtaḥ ca sat mune iti śrī śivamahā sa krodhaḥ prāha giriśaḥ vīrabhadra ādikān gaṇān he vīrabhadra he naṃdin kṣetrapāla aṣṭa bhairavāḥ gaṇa aśva sannaddhāḥ sa āyudhāḥ bala śālinaḥ kumārābhyām saha eva adya nirgacchantu mama ājñayā śaṃkhacūḍa va dhārthāya nirgacchāmi adya sa tvaram iti ājñāpya maheśānaḥ niryayau sainya saṃyutaḥ sarve vīra gaṇāḥ tasya anuyayuḥ saṃpraharṣitāḥ ityādayaḥ gaṇa īśānāḥ sainyānām patayaḥ varāḥ teṣām ca gaṇanām vacmi sāvadhāna tayā śṛṇu śaṃkha karṇaḥ koṭi gaṇaiḥ yutaḥ para vimardakaḥ devaḥ bhṛṃgī kaṃkālaḥ kālakaḥ kālaḥ nandī sarvāntakaḥ tathā yuddha artham niryayuḥ prītyā śaṃkhacūḍena nirbhayāḥ rudra akṣa ābharaṇāḥ sarve tathā asat bhasma dhāriṇaḥ sūrya koṭi pratīkāśāḥ pravīṇāḥ yuddha karmaṇi pṛthivī cāriṇaḥ kecid kecid pātāla cāriṇaḥ ugradaṃṣṭra vāyadaṇḍaḥ koraṭaḥ koṭabhaḥ tathā mahāvīram ca sauram ca kālakālam mahānalam taiḥ ca eva abhivṛtaḥ skaṃdaḥ praṇamya candraśekharam atha śaṃbhuḥ samānīya sva sainyam sakalam tadā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadhe mahādevayuddhayātrāvarṇanam nāma trayodaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ dhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca vidhitāta mahābuddhe mune jīva ciram samāḥ śaṅkhacūḍaḥ uvāca śambhu dūta mukhāt devi yuddhāya aham samudyataḥ iti evam uktvā sa jñānī nānā bodhanataḥ priyām nānā kāma kalābhiḥ ca niśi cāṭu śataiḥ api brāhme muhūrte utthāya prātar kṛtyam vidhāya ca priyām āśvāsayāmāsa sa rājā rudatīm punar niṣedhantīm ca gamanam nānā vārttā prakathya ca nija senāpatim vīram samāhūya samāhataḥ śaṅkhacūḍaḥ uvāca adya senāpate vīrāḥ sarve samara śālinaḥ daityāḥ ca dānavāḥ śūrāḥ ṣaḍaśītiḥ udāyudhāḥ kaṅkānām balinām śīghram senā niryātu nirbhayāḥ pañcāśat asurāṇām hi nirgacchantu kulāni vai koṭi vīryāṇi yuddha artham śambhunā deva pakṣiṇā sannaddhāni ca ghoṣrāṇām kulāni ca śatam drutam nirgacchantu raṇa artham hi śambhunā mama śāsanāt kālakeyāḥ ca mauryāḥ ca dauhṛdāḥ kālakāḥ tathā sajjāḥ niryāntu yuddhāya rudreṇa mama śāsanāt iti ājñāpya surapatiḥ dānava indraḥ mahā balaḥ tri lakṣa akṣauhiṇī yuktaḥ māṇḍalyam ca cakāra ha bahis babhūva śibirāt raṇe vīra bhayaṃkaraḥ kapilasya tapaḥ sthānam puṇya kṣetram ca bhārate puṣpabhadrā nadī ramyā jala pūrṇā sarasvatī iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe śaṅkha dūtam kṛtvā mahā vijñam preṣayā māsa śaṃkaram sa tatra gatvām dūtaḥ ca candra bhālam dadarśa ha śaṃkaram sa kumāram ca śirasā praṇanāma saḥ athā asau śaṃkhacūḍasya dūtaḥ parama śāstra vid uvāca śaṃkaram natvā karau baddhā śubham vacaḥ dūtaḥ uvāca śaṃkhacūḍasya dūtaḥ aham tvad sakāśam iha āgataḥ iti śrutvā ca vacanam śaṃkhacūḍasya śaṃkaraḥ prasanna ātmā mahādevaḥ bhagavān tam u vāca ha mahādevaḥ uvāca kathanīyām idam tasmai nirvivādam vicārya ca tasya tvam tanayaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ dharma ātmā dānava īśvaraḥ dānavīm yonim āyātaḥ tattvataḥ na hi dānavaḥ nija vṛttam purā jñātvā deva varam tyaja adhunā na adhikam sa vikāram ca kuru rājyam vicārya ca nija rājye sukham tiṣṭha tiṣṭhatu sva pade surāḥ kulīnāḥ śuddha karmāṇaḥ sarve kaśyapa vaṃśa jāḥ ityādi bahuvāttau ca śruti smṛti parām śubhām śikṣita śaṃkhacūḍena sa dūtaḥ tarka vittamaḥ dūtaḥ uvāca tat kim īśa asurāṇām ca na surāṇām vada prabho śiraḥ chedam cakāra asau kasmāt cakrī maheśvara bhavān cakāra giriśaḥ surapakṣī iti viśrutam tava etat viparītam ca manasā saṃvicāryatām maheśaḥ uvāca vayam bhakta para adhīnāḥ na svataṃtrāḥ kadāpi hi tad icchayā tad karmāṇaḥ na kasya api ca pakṣiṇaḥ prahlāda artha vadhaḥ akāri bhaktānām hita kāriṇā deva prārthanayā akāri mayā api ca purā śrutam sarva īśvaryāḥ sarva mātu deva prārthanayā purā āsīt śubha ādibhiḥ yuddham vadhaḥ teṣām tayā kṛtaḥ hari brahma ādikānām ca prārthanā vaśatā upi aham surāṇām amī śvaraḥ dūta yuddha artham agamam khalu devakayā arthim īśaḥ aham vinayena ca preṣitaḥ gaccha tvam śaṃkhacūḍe vai kathanīyam ca me vacaḥ iti uktvā śaṃkaraḥ tatra virarāma maheśvaraḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpu rāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadhe śivadūtasaṃvādaḥ nāma paṃcatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ gatvā ca śiva vākyam jagāda ha tat śrutvā śaṃkhacūḍā asau dānava indraḥ pratāpavān aṃgīcakāra su prītyā raṇam eva sa dānavaḥ ādideśa sva sainyam ca yuddha artha śaṃkareṇa ca yuddha āraṃbhaḥ babhūva āśu neduḥ vādyāni bhūriśas saṃjātaḥ vīra śabdaḥ tathā eva ca gokāmukhena cūrṇena khaḍga nānā sureṇa ca dhūmreṇa saṃhalena api viśvena ca pratāpinā palāśena dvādaśā arkāḥ yuyudhuḥ dharmataḥ pare asuraiḥ amarāḥ sārddham śiva sāhāyya śālinaḥ rāhuṇā saha candraśva jīvaḥ śukreṇa dharmataḥ nānā āyudhāni divyāni calati sma mahā mṛdhe gadaṣṭi paṭṭiśāḥ cakra bhuśuṃḍi prāsa mudgarāḥ nitriṃśa bhalla parighāḥ śaktayaḥ unmukha paraśvadhāḥ śara tomara khaṅgāḥ ca śatadbhyaḥ ca sahasraśas bhidipāla ādayaḥ ca anye vīra hasteṣu śobhitāḥ śirāṃsi cicchiduḥ ca ebhiḥ varāḥ tatra mahā utsavāḥ vīrāṇām ubhayoḥ ca eva sainyayoḥ garjatoḥ raṇe gajāḥ turaṃgāḥ bahavaḥ syandanāḥ ca padātayaḥ sa āroha vāhāḥ vividhāḥ taṃtra āsan su vikhaṃḍitāḥ nikṛtta bāhu ūru kara kaṭi karṇa yuga aṃtrayaḥ saṃchinna dhvaja bāṇa asi tanutra vara bhūṣaṇāḥ samu ddhata kirīṭaiḥ ca śirobhiḥ saha kuṃḍalaiḥ saṃraṃbha naṣṭeḥ āstīrṇā babhau bhūḥ karabha ūrubhiḥ sahasā paṭalaiḥ vā sa sāraṃghaiḥ mṛdhe bhaṭāḥ pradhāvantaḥ kabaṃdhān sva śiraḥ ukṣibhiḥ tarām vīrāḥ yuyudhuḥ ca parasparam vyanadan vīra saṃnnāda sa toyāḥ iva toyadāḥ āhvayantaḥ viśaṃtaḥ aye kṣipataḥ marmabhiḥ mithas sarvatas vīra saṃghāḥ ca nānā bāhu dhvaja āyudhāḥ vyadṛśyaṃta mahā saṃkhye kurvataḥ siṃha saṃravam sva śakhān ca vidadhmuḥ vai pṛthak pṛthak balganam cakrire taṃtra mahā vīrāḥ praharṣitāḥ babhūva yuddham vikaṭam karālam vīraṃharṣa dam līlā iyam śaṃkarasya parātmanaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadhe parasparayuddhavarṇanam nāma paṭtriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ dudruvuḥ bhaya bhītāḥ ca śastra astra kṣata vigrahāḥ te parāvṛtya viśveśam śaṃkaram śaraṇam yayuḥ trāhi trāhi iti sarva īśa iti ūcuḥ vihvalayā girā dṛṣṭvā parājayam teṣām deva ādīnām sa śaṃkaraḥ sa bhayam vacanam śrutvā kopam uccais cakāra ha nirakṣyi sa kṛpā dṛṣṭayā devebhyaḥ śvā abhayam dadau śiva ājñaptaḥ tadā skandaḥ dānavānām gaṇaiḥ saha kṛtvā krodham vīra śabdam devaḥ yaḥ tārakāṃtakaḥ teṣām śirāṃsi saṃchidya babhakṣa sahasā ca sā papau raktāni teṣām ca dānavānām samaṃtataḥ yuddham cakāra vividham sura dānava bhīṣaṇam kabaṃdhānām sahasram ca san nanarta raṇe bahu mahān kolāhalaḥ jātaḥ kkībānām ca bhayaṃkaraḥ punar skaṃdaḥ prakupya uccais śara varṣī cakāra ha pātayāmāsa kṣayataḥ koṭiśas asura nāyakān dānavāḥ śara jālena skandasya kṣata vigrahāḥ bhītāḥ praduvuḥ sarve śeṣāḥ mara ṇataḥ tadā mahā mārī ca yuyudhe na babhūva parāṅmukhī babhūvuḥ te kṣata aṃga aśva skaṃda śakti prapīḍitāḥ mahāmārī skaṃdayoḥ ca vijayaḥ bhūt tadā sune mahā mārīkṛtam tat ca upadravam kṣaya hetukam cukopa atīva sahasā saṃnaddhaḥ bhūt svayam tadā varam vimānam āruhya nānā śastra astra saṃyutam mahāvīraiḥ śaṃkhacūḍaḥ jagāma dhanuḥ vikṛṣya karṇa antam cakāra śara varṣaṇam tad dṛṣṭayā prahataḥ skandaḥ babhūva śiva nandanaḥ nānāvidhā sva māyām ca cakāra maya darśitām tām na avidan surāḥ kepi gaṇāḥ ca muni sattama mūrcchām avāpa sahasā tad prahāreṇa sa kṣaṇa m punar ca cetanām prāpya kārtikaḥ para vīra hā smṛtvā pādau maheśasya sa ambi kasya ca ṣaṇmukhaḥ vīra śabdam cakāra asau jagarja ca punar punar muhūrta mātram tad kleśam vinīya sa mahā balaḥ śaktyā jaghāna tam ca api kārti keyam mahā balam sa papāta mahī pṛṣṭhe moghām kurvan vidhi pradām jñānena tam śivaḥ ca api jīva yāmāsa līlayā śaṃkhacūḍena yuyudhe samare bala śālinā vavarṣa samare astrāṇi yāni yāni ca dānavaḥ ciccheda līlayā vīraḥ tānitāni nija śaraiḥ athā atīva cukopa uccais śakha cūḍaḥ pratāpavān śaktyā jaghāna urasi tam sa cakaṃpe patāta kau jagrāha ca dhanuḥ bhūyas vīrabhadraḥ gaṇāgraṇīḥ bhakṣitum dānavān rakṣitum kārtika icchayā punar samudyatāḥ ca āsan vīrā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadhe sa sainya śaṃkhacūḍayuddhavarṇanam nāma saptatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ sā ca gatvā hi saṃgrāmam siṃhanādam cakāra ha devyāḥ ca tena nādena mūcchīm āpuḥ ca dānavāḥ anyāḥ ca devyaḥ tatra ājau nanṛtuḥ madhu saṃpaṣuḥ mahān kolāhalaḥ jātaḥ gaṇadeva dale tadā vṛddhim jagāma tat chatram dṛṣṭvā vāmam ca dānavam nivṛttim prāpa tat chatram dṛṣṭvā namram ca dānavam tam dṛṣṭvā prajjvalaṃtam ca praṇamya bhuvi saṃsthitaḥ atha kruddhaḥ dānava indraḥ dhanuḥ ākṛṣya raṃhasā jagarja sa aṭṭahāsam ca dānavāḥ bhayam āyayuḥ kālyai cikṣepa śaktim sa śata yojanam āyatām sa ca vaiṣṇavam astram ca cikṣepa caṃḍikā upari māheśvareṇa kālī ca vinivāram cakāra sā prekṣakāḥ abhavana sarve devāḥ ca dānavāḥ api atha kruddhā mahā devī kālī kāla samā raṇe na kṣipa astram imam devi śaṃkhacūḍāya vai ruṣā mṛtyuḥ pāśupatāt moghāt api ca caṃḍike śaṃkhacūḍasya vīrasya upāyam anyam vicāraya grīṣma sūrya upamam tīkṣṇa dhāram atyanta bhīkaram sā kālī tam samālokya āyatim prajjvalitam ruṣā prasārya mukham āhāram cakre tasya ca paśyataḥ divyāni astrāṇi ca anyāni ciccheda dānava īśvaraḥ prāptāni pūrvatas cakre śata khaṃḍāni tāni ca vegena muṣṭinā kālī tam a dṛṣṭvā ca dānavam athā aṃgatya drutam māyī cakram cikṣepa vegataḥ bhadrakālyai śaṃkhacūḍaḥ pralaya agni śikhā upamam sā devī tam tadā cakram vāma hastena līlayā jagrāha sva mukhena eva āhāram cakre ruṣā drutam babhrāḥ ma dānava indraḥ upi kṣaṇam mad śam avāpa saḥ na cakre bāhu yuddham ca mātṛ buddhayā tayā saha aruroha sa hṛṣṭa tmā na bhrāntaḥ api mahaḥ raṇe uddhata sārddham tatas tvam bhuṃkṣva ca īśvari saṃgrāme dānava indram ca hetum na kuru mānasam avadhyaḥ yam śaṃkhacūḍaḥ tava devī iti niścayam tat śrutvā vacanam devī niḥsṛtam vyoma maṃḍalāt vyāsaḥ uvāca śrutvā kālī uktam īśāno kim cakāra kim uktavān mahā līlā karaḥ śaṃbhar jahāsa āśvāsayan ca tām mahāvṛṣabham ārūḍhaḥ vīrabhadra ādi saṃyutaḥ raṇam prāptaḥ maheśaḥ ca vīra rūpam vidhāya ca virarāja adhikam tatra rudraḥ mūrtaḥ iva antakaḥ tam praṇamyam tu yogena vimānam āruroha saḥ bāṇa varṣam iva uyam tat vaṣa tomaghayoḥ tadā śaṃkhacūḍaḥ mahā vīraḥ śarām vikṣepa dāruṇān ciccheda śaṃkaraḥ tān vai līlayā sva śaṃra utkaraiḥ tad aṃgeṣu ca śastra oghaiḥ tāḍayāmāsa kopataḥ dānavaḥ niśitam khaṅgam carma ca ādāya vegavān vṛṣam jaghāna śirasi śivasya vara vāhanam tāḍite vāhane rudraḥ tam kṣurapreṇa lī layā chinne asau carmaṇi tadā śaktim cikṣepa saḥ asuraḥ dvidhā cakre sva bāṇena haraḥ tām saṃmukhā gatām kopa ādhmātaḥ śaṃkhacūḍaḥ cakram cikṣepa dānavaḥ gadām āvidhya tarasā saṃcikṣepa haram prati śaṃkhanā sā api sahasā bhinnā bhasma tvam āgatā dhāvati sma haram vegāt śaṃkhacūḍaḥ kudhā kulaḥ samāhṛtya sva bāṇa oghaiḥ apātayata śaṃkaraḥ drutam paraśu hastam tam bhū tale līlayā asuram tatas kṣaṇane saṃprāpya saṃjñām āruhya sat ratham dhṛta divya āyudha śaraḥ babhau vyāpya akhilam nabhaḥ āyatam tam nirīkṣya eva ḍamaru dhvānam ādarāt cakāra jyā ravam ca api dhanuṣaḥ dussaham haraḥ pu rayāmāsa kakubhaḥ śṛṃga nādena ca prabhuḥ svayam jagarja giriśaḥ trāsayan asurān tadā tyājite bha mahā garvaiḥ mahā nādaiḥ vṛṣeśvaraḥ pūrayāmāsa sahasā kham gām vasu diśaḥ tathā mahākālaḥ samutpatya tāḍayat gām tathā nabhaḥ mahā kolāhalaḥ jātaḥ raṇa madhye bhayaṃkaraḥ vīra śabdaḥ babhūva atha gaṇa madhye samaṃtataḥ cukopa atīva tat śrutvā dānava indraḥ mahā balaḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti duṣṭa ātman vyājahāra yadām haraḥ śakti cikṣepa rudrāya jvālā mālā ati bhīṣaṇām vahni kūṭa prabhāyatī kṣetrapālena satvaram cakape dharaṇī sa naga andhi jalāśayā dām bhimuktān śarāśam hitān sa ca sahasraśas śaraiḥ uyaśviccheda śataśas tadā tatas śaṃbhuḥ triśūlena saṃkuddhaḥ tam jaghāna ha tad prahāram sahya āśu kau papāta sa mūcchiṃtaḥ tatas kṣaṇena saṃprāpa saṃjñām sa ca tadā suraḥ ājaghāna śaraiḥ rudram tān sarvān ātta kārmukaḥ bāhūnām ayutam kṛtvā chādayāmāsa śaṃkaram tatas vegena sahasā gadām ādāya dānavaḥ gadām ciccheda tasya aśvā āpa tatas saḥ asinā haraḥ śita dhāreṇa saṃkruddhaḥ duṣṭa garva apahārakaḥ chinnāyām svargadāyām ca cukopa atīva dānavaḥ śūlam jagrāha tejasvī pareṣām dussaham jvalat sudarśanam śūla hastam āyāṃtam dānava īśvaram sva triśūlena vivyādha hṛdi tam vegataḥ haraḥ triśūla bhinna hṛdayāt niṣkrāṃtaḥ puruṣaḥ paraḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭhati ca uvāca vyavārṣaḥ śaṃkhacūḍasya vīryavān asurān tān bahūn krodhāt prasārtham sva mukham tadā kṣetrapālaḥ cakhāda anyān bahūn daityān kudhā ākulaḥ vīrabhadraḥ aparān vīrān bahūn krodhāt anāśayat naṃdīśvaraḥ jaghāna anyān bahūn amara mardakān evam bahu gaṇāḥ vīrāḥ tadā saṃnahya kopataḥ vidvatāśva apare vīrāḥ bahavaḥ bhaya kātarāḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍasainyavadhavarṇanam nāma navatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ sva balam nihatam dṛṣṭvā mukhyam bahutaram tatas tathā vīrān prāṇa samān cukopa atīva dānavaḥ uvā ca vacanam śaṃbhum tiṣṭhāmi ājau sthiraḥ bhava agacchat niścayam kṛtvā abhimukham śaṃkarasya ca divyāni astrāṇi cikṣepa mahārudrāya dānavaḥ cakāra śara vṛṣṭim ca toya vṛṣṭim yathā ghanaḥ māyāḥ cakāra vividhāḥ adṛśyāḥ bhaya darśitāḥ tām dṛṣṭvā śaṃkaraḥ tatra cikṣepa astram ca līlayā tejasā tasya tad māyā naṣṭa āśvāsana tadā śūlam jagrāha sahasā durnivārya su tejasām kṣipa śūlam na ca idānīm prārthanām śṛṇu śaṃkara sarvathā tvam hi kṣaṇāda brahmāṇḍa nāśane kim eka dānavasya īśa śaṃkhacūḍasya sāṃpratam tathā api veda maryādā na nāśyā svāminā tvayā tām śṛṇuṣva mahādeva saphalam kuru satyataḥ tāvat asya jarā mṛtyuḥ śakhacaḍasya śaṃkara iti ākarṇya nabhaḥ vāṇīm tathā iti ukte hare tadā hara icchayā gataḥ vi snuḥ tam dideśa satām gatiḥ śaṃkhacūḍa upakaṃṭham ca gatvā uvāca sa tam tadā vṛddha brāhmaṇaḥ uvāca na idānīm kathayiṣyāmi prakaṭam dīna vatsalam tat adau kavacam divyam viprāya prāṇa saṃmatam śaṃkhacūḍasya gatvā tatra hariḥ tasyāḥ yonau māyā viśāradaḥ vīrya ādhānam cakāra āśu deva kārya artham īśvaraḥ śaṃkhacūḍa vadha arthāya śūlam jagrāha prajvalat tat śūlam vijayam nāma śaṃkarasya parātmanaḥ saṃcakāśe diśaḥ sarvāḥ rodasī saṃprakāśayat vibhramat vyomni tat śūlam śaṃkhacūḍa upari kṣaṇāt cakāra bhasma tat śītram nipatya śiva śāsanāt atha śūlam maheśasya drutam āvṛtya śaṃkaram yayaiau vihāyasā viprāḥ manaḥ yāyi sva kārya kṛt tuṣṭuvuḥ munayaḥ devāḥ nanṛtuḥ ca apsaraḥ gaṇāḥ praśaśaṃsa hariḥ brahmā śakra ādyāḥ munayaḥ tathā śaṃkhacūḍaḥ dānava indraḥ śivasya kṛpayā tadā śāpa muktaḥ babhūva tha pūrva rūpam avāpa ha viśeṣeṇa hare lakṣmyāḥ śaṃkha toyam mahat priyam saṃbaṃdhinām ca tasya api na harasya mahā mune tam ittham śaṃkaraḥ hatvā śiva lokam jagāma saḥ su prahṛṣṭaḥ vṛṣa ārūḍhaḥ soma skanda gaṇaiḥ vṛtaḥ hariḥ jagāma vaikuṃṭham kṛṣṇaḥ svasthaḥ babhūva ha sarva duḥkha haram śrī sarva kāma prapūrakam śrāvayet vā paṭhet vā api pāṭhayet vā sudhīḥ naraḥ dhana āḍhyaḥ vaiśya jaḥ śūdra śṛṇvan sattama tām iyāt iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍavadho pākhyānam nāma catvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca nārāyaṇaḥ hi devānām kārya kartā satām gatiḥ śaṃkhacūḍasya rūpeṇa reme tad ramayā saha tat eva śṛṇu viṣṇoḥ śva caritam pramudā āvaham śiva śāsana kartuḥ ca mātuḥ ca jagatām hareḥ raṇa madhye vyoma vacaḥ śrutvā devena śaṃbhunā preritaḥ śaṃkhacūḍasya gṛhītvā kavacam param vipra rūpeṇa tvaritam māyayā nijayā hariḥ jagāma śaṃkhacūḍasya rūpeṇa tulasī gṛham drutam cakāra śṛṃgāram jñātvā a yātam nijam patim śaṃkhacūḍa sva rūpaḥ sa māyāvī deva kārya kṛt iti uktvā sa kaṭākṣam sā nirīkṣya sa smitam mudā yasya ājñā varttinaḥ devāḥ viṣṇu brahma ādayaḥ sadā kailāsa vāsī gaṇapaḥ para brahma satām gatiḥ viṣṇu brahma ādayaḥ atītāḥ bahavaḥ kṣaṇa mātrataḥ katham babhūva saṃgrāmaḥ tena deva sahāyinā kuśalī tvam iha āyātaḥ tam jitvā parameśvaram śrutvā ittham tulasī vākyam sa vihasya ramāpatiḥ bhagavān uvāca yadā aham raṃṇa bhūmau ca jagāma samara priyaḥ kolāhalaḥ mahān yātaḥ pravṛttaḥ abhūt mahā raṇaḥ deva dānavayoḥ yuddham saṃbabhūva jaya eṣiṇoḥ tadā aham samaram tatra akāryam devaiḥ bala utkaṭaiḥ rudraḥ api tad sahāya artham ājagāma raṇam prati sarva svāsthyam atīva āpa hi upadravaḥ kaḥ vā tvam vada tat dṛṣṭvā tulasī rūpam jñātvā viṣṇum tu cihnataḥ pātitratya parityāgāt kruddhā sā tam uvāca ha pāṣāṇa sadṛśaḥ tvam ca dayā hīnaḥ yatas khalaḥ ye vadaṃti dayā sindhum tvām bhrāṃtāḥ te na saṃśayaḥ iti ukttā tulasī sā vai śaṃkhacūḍa priyā satī tatas tām rudatīm dṛṣṭvā sa viṣṇuḥ parameśvaraḥ sasmāra śaṃkaram devam yena saṃmohitam jagat tatas prādurbabhūvātha śaṃkaraḥ bhakta vatsalaḥ hariṇā praṇataḥ ca āsīt saṃnutaḥ vinayena saḥ śoka ākulam harim dṛṣṭvā vilapantīm ca tad priyām nayena bodhayāmāsa tam tām kṛpaṇa vatsalām śaṃkaraḥ uvāca prastutam śṛṇu nirduḥkham śṛṇoti su manāḥ hariḥ ramastra hariṇā nityam ramayā sadṛśī bhava tava iyam tanuḥ utsṛṣṭā nadī rūpā bhavet iha kiyatkālam mahādevi deva pūjanam sādhane pradhāna rūpā tulasī bhaviṣyati vareṇa me vṛkṣa adhiṣṭhātṛ devī tvam vaikuṃṭhe divya rūpiṇī sārddham rahasi hariṇā nityam krīḍām kariṣyasi hariḥ vai śaila rūpī ca gaṃḍakī tīra saṃnidhau tatra koṭayāḥ ca tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭrāḥ bhayaṃkarāḥ śālagrāmaśilā sā hi tad bhedāt ati puṇya dā lakṣmī nārāyaṇa ākhyā ādi śvakra bhedāt bhaviṣyati śālagrāma śilā viṣṇo tulasyāḥ tava saṃgamaḥ sadā sādṛśya rūpā yā bahu puṇya vivarddhinī iti uktvā śaṃkaraḥ tatra māhātmyam ūcivān tadā śālagrāma śilāyāḥ ca tulasyāḥ bahu puṇya dam tatas ca aṃtarhitaḥ bhūtvā modayitvā harim ca tām jagāma sva ālayam śaṃbhuḥ śarma daḥ hi sadā satām iti śrutvā vacaḥ śaṃbhoḥ prasannā tu tulasya bhūt śailaḥ bhūt acyutaḥ saḥ api tad tīre puṇya daḥ nṛṇām jale patanti yāḥ tatra śilāḥ tāḥ tu ati puṇya dāḥ ākhyānam idam ākhyātam viṣṇu māhātmya miśritam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudra saṃhitāyām payuddha khaṃḍe śaṃkhacūḍa vadha upākhyāne tulasīśāpavarṇanam nāma ekacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ nāradaḥ uvāca śaṃkhacūḍa vadham śrutvā caritam śaśimauli naḥ ayam tṛptaḥ asmi no tvattaḥ amṛtam pītvā yathā janaḥ māyām āśritya saṅghīlām kurvataḥ bhakta moda dām aprākṣīt imam eva artham brahma putram sunīśvaram sanatkumāraḥ provāca vyāsam satyavatī sutam su praśaṃsya maheśasya caritam maṃgalāyanam śṛṇu vyāsa maheśasya caritam maṃgalāyanam yathā andhakaḥ gāṇapatyam prāpa śaṃbhoḥ parātmanaḥ kṛtvā parama saṃgrāmam tena pūrvam sunīśvara prasādya tam maheśānam sattva bhāvāt punar punar māhātmyam adbhutam śaṃbho śaraṇa āgata rakṣiṇaḥ su bhakta vatsalasya eva nānā līlā vihāriṇaḥ vacaḥ mahārtham praṇipatya bhaktyā hi uvāca tam brahma sutam muni iṃdram vyāsaḥ uvāca jātaḥ mahātmā balavān pradhānaḥ kimātmakaḥ kasya sutaḥ adhakaḥ ca gāṇapatyam katham prāpa śaṃbhoḥ parama tejasaḥ soṃdhakaḥ dhanyaḥ eva ati yaḥ babhūva gaṇeśvaraḥ maheśvara ūtīḥ parama āpta lakṣmīḥ saṃśrotu kāmam janakam śukasya purā āgataḥ bhakta kṛpā karaḥ asau kailāsataḥ śailam sutā gaṇa āḍhyaḥ vihartu kāmaḥ kila kāśikām vai sva śailataḥ nirjara cakravartī sa rājadhānīm ca vidhāya tasyām cakre parotīḥ sukha dāḥ janānām tad rakṣakam bhairava nāma vīram kṛtvā samam śailajayā hi bahvīḥ tatra api nānā gaṇa vīra mukhyaiḥ śivā sametaḥ vijahāra bhūri pravāla hema abja dhṛta prabhābhyām kara ambujābhyām nimimīla netre harasya netreṣu nimīliteṣu kṣaṇena jātaḥ su mahā aṃdhakāraḥ tad sparśa yogāt ca maheśvarasya karau ca tasyāḥ skhalitam mada aṃbhaḥ andhaḥ virūpī jaṭilaḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ naretaraḥ vaikṛtikaḥ su romā śrī maheśaḥ uvāca nimīlya netrāṇi kṛtam ca karma bibheṣi sā asmāt dayite katham tvam jāte prakāśe sati ghora rūpaḥ jātaḥ udhakārāt api netra hīnaḥ tādṛgvidham tam ca nirīkṣya bhūtam papraccha gaurī puruṣam maheśam śrutvā haraḥ tat vacanam priyāyāḥ līlākaraḥ sṛṣṭi kṛtaḥ adharūpām līlākarāya atri jagat jananyā vihasya kiṃcid bhagavān uvāca maheśaḥ uvāca bike hi adbhuta vṛtta kāre utpannaḥ eṣaḥ adbhuta caṃḍa vīryaḥ tvam ca asya kartāsi ayathānurūpam tvayā āsa sakhyā dayayā gaṇebhyaḥ sa rakṣitavyaḥ tvayi tam hi vā ekam vicārya buddha akaraṇīya māyeṃ gaurī tatas bhṛtya vacaḥ niśamya kāruṇya bhāvāt sahitā sakhībhiḥ nānā prakāraiḥ bahubhiḥ yupāyaiḥ cakāra rakṣām sva sutasya yadvat sva jyeṣṭha baṃdhoḥ stanaya pratānam saṃvīkṣya ca āsīt priyayā niyuktaḥ araṇyam āśritya tapaḥ cakāra asuraḥ tadā kaśyapa jaḥ stuta artham kāṣṭha upamaḥ asau jita roṣa doṣaḥ saṃdarśana artham tu maheśvarasya tuṣṭaḥ pinākī tapasā asya samyak vara pradānāya yayau dvijendraḥ tat sthānam ā sādya vṛṣadhvajaḥ asau jagāda daitya pravaram maheśaḥ madeśaḥ uvāca sarasyam ākarṇya maheśa vākyam hi ati prasannaḥ kanakākṣa daityaḥ kṛtāṃjali namra śirāḥ uvāca stutyā ca natvā vividham girīśam hiraṇyākṣaḥ uvāca putraḥ tu me candralalāṭa nā asti su vīryavān daitya kula anurūpīm yasmāt ca mad dhātuḥ anaṃta vīryāḥ prahlāda pūrve api paṃca putrāḥ mama iha nā asti iti gata anvayaḥ ham kaḥ māmakam rājyam idam bubhūṣet rājyam parasya sva balena hṛtvā bhuṃkte athavā svam pituḥ eva dṛṣṭam urddham gatiḥ putravatām niruktā manīṣibhiḥ dharma bhṛtām variṣṭhaiḥ sadā samārādhya surātri paṃkajam yācaṃte ittham sutam ekam eva mama ātmajam tu aṃdhaka nāmadheyam tvad tulya vīryam tu aparājitam ca vṛṇīṣva putram sakalam vihāya duḥkham pratīcchasva sutam tvam eva tatas harāt prāpya sutam sa daityaḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya yathākrameṇa stotraiḥ anekaiḥ abhipūjya rudram tuṣṭaḥ sva rājyam gatavān mahātmā tatas tu putram giriśāt avāpya rasātalam caṃḍa parā kramaḥ tu imām dharitrīm anayat sva deśam daityaḥ vijitvā tridaśān aśeṣān vārāham āśritya vapuḥ pradhānam ārādhitaḥ viṣṇuḥ anaṃta vīryaḥ ghoṇā prahāraiḥ vividhaiḥ dharitrīm vidāryam pātāla talam praviśya tuṃḍena daityān śataśas vicūrṇya daṃṣṭrābhiḥ agryā bhiḥ akhaṃḍitābhiḥ pāda prahāraiḥ aśani prakāśaiḥ unmathya sainyāni niśācarāṇām mārtaṃḍa koṭi pratimena paścāt sudarśanena adbhuta caṃḍa tejāḥ hiraṇyanetrasya śiraḥ jvalaṃtam ciccheda daityān ca dadāha duṣṭān tatas prahṛṣṭaḥ ditija indra rājaḥ tvam aṃdhakam tatra sa ca abhyaṣiṃcat sva sthānam āgatya tatas dharitrīm dṛṣṭvā aṃkureṇa uddharataḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ bhūmim ca pātāla talāt mahātmā pupoṣa bhāgam tu aṃtha pūrvakam tu hiraṇyanetre atha hate asura īśe varāha rūpeṇa sureṇa devāḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe hiraṇyākṣavadhaḥ nāma dvicatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca kim akārṣīt tatas tasya jyeṣṭha bhrātā mahā asuraḥ kutūhalam iti śrotum mama asti iha muni īśvaraḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ vyāsa śucā nirdiḥ daśa asurān vīrān hari vaira priyaḥ hi saḥ dhātre vijñāpayāmāsuḥ tat tapaḥ vikṛta ānanāḥ parītaḥ bhṛgu dakṣa ādyaiḥ yayau daitya īśvara āśramam varam hi dātum tam uvāca dhātā varam vṛṇīṣva iti pitāmahaḥ upi niśamya vācam madhurām vidhātuḥ vacaḥ abravīt evam amūḍha buddhiḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ uvāca śastra astra pāśa aśani śuṣka vṛkṣa giri indra toya agni ripu prahāraiḥ svarge dharaṇyām divase niśāyām na eva ūrddhataḥ na api adhataḥ prajā īśa praṇamya viṣṇum manasā tam āha dayā anvitaḥ asau iti padmayoniḥ alam tapaḥ te paripūrṇa kāmaḥ samāḥ sahasrāṇi ca ṣaṇṇavatyaḥ uttiṣṭha rājyam kuru dānavānām śrutvā giram tad su mukhaḥ babhūva rājya abhiṣiktaḥ prapitāmahena trailokya nāśāya mati cakāra tatas bhayāt iṃdra mukhāḥ ca devāḥ pitāmaha ājñām samavāpya sarve upadrutāḥ daitya vareṇa jātāḥ kṣīrodadhim yatra hariḥ tu śete ārādhayāmāsuḥ atīva viṣṇum stutvā vacobhiḥ sukha dam hi matvā śrutvā tadīyam sakalam hi duḥkham tuṣṭaḥ rameśaḥ pradadau varān tu āśvāsya devān akhilān munīn vā uvāca vaiśvānara tulya tejāḥ daityam haniṣye prasabham sura īśāḥ prayāta dhāmāni nijāni tuṣṭāḥ śrutvā rameśasya vacaḥ tura īśāḥ śakra ādikāḥ te nikhilā api tuṣṭāḥ yayuḥ sva dhāmāni hiraṇyanetra anujam ca matvā nihatam sunīśa āśritya loḍa yai luṅ ārṣaḥ prathamā ārṣī rūpam jaṭilam karālam daṃṣṭra āyudham tīkṣṇa nakham su nāsam saiṃham ca nāram su vidārita āsyam mārtaṃḍa koṭi pratimam su ghoram yugāṃta kāla agni sama prabhāvam jagat mayam kim bahubhiḥ vacobhiḥ astam khau asaḥ api hi gacchati īśaḥ gataḥ asurāṇām nagarīm mahātmā kṛtvā ca yuddham prabalaiḥ sa daityaiḥ hatvā atha tān daitya gaṇān gṛhītvā babhrāma tatra adbhuta vikramaḥ ca vabhaṃja tān tān asurān nṛsiṃhaḥ siṃham ca tam sarva mayam nirīkṣya prahlāda nāmā ditija indra putraḥ uvāca rājānam ayam mṛgendrī jagat mayaḥ kim samupāgataḥ ca prahlādaḥ uvāca eṣa praviṣṭaḥ bhagavān anaṃtaḥ nṛsiṃha mātraḥ nagaram tvad aṃtar nivṛtya yuddhāt śaraṇam prayāhi paśyāmi siṃhasya karāla mūrttim uktvā iti putram ditija adhināthaḥ daitya ṛṣabhān vīra varān sa rājā vai siṃha m amum bhavantaḥ vīrāḥ virūpa bhrukuṭi īkṣaṇam tu tasya ājñayā daitya varāḥ tatas te grahītu kāmāḥ viviśuḥ mṛgendram kṣaṇena dagdhāḥ śalabhāḥ iva agnim rūpa abhi lāṣāt pravivikṣavaḥ vai śastraiḥ samayaiḥ ā khilaiḥ tathā astraiḥ śakti aṣṭi pāśa aṃkuśa pāvaka aṃdyaiḥ saṃyudhyatoḥ eva tayoḥ jagāma brāhmam dinam vyāsa hi śastra pāṇyoḥ tatas sa daityaḥ saḥ isā vahūn ca kṛtvā bhujān śastra yutān nirīkṣya nṛsiṃha rūpam prayayau mṛgendram saṃyudhyamānam sahasā samaṃtāt kṛtvā mahā daitya varaḥ nṛsiṃham kṣayam gataiḥ śūla dharaḥ abhyupāyāt nidhāya jānau sa bhujāṃtareṣu nakha aṃkuraiḥ dānava marma bhidbhiḥ nakha astra hṛd padmam asṛj vimiśram utpādya jīvāt vigataḥ kṣaṇena tyaktaḥ tadānīm sa tu kāṣṭha bhūtaḥ punar punar varṇita sarva gātraḥ tatas ati hṛṣṭāḥ sakalāḥ sura īśāḥ praṇamya viṣṇum diśi vipra tasyām yayuḥ svadhā mānim pitāmaha ādyāḥ kṛta sva kāryam bhaga vaṃtam īḍyam nṛsiṃhataḥ tad sahajasya nāśaḥ prahāda rājya āptiḥ iti prasaṃgāt śṛṇu tu idānīm dvija varya mattaḥ udhaka prabhāvam bhava kṛt pralabdham hareṇa yuddham khalu tasya paścāt gaṇa ādhipatyam tasya iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe gāṇādhipataprātyaṃdhakajanmahiraṇyanetrahiraṇyakaśipuvadhavarṇanam nāma tvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ tatas hiraṇyākṣa sutaḥ kadācid saṃśrāvitaḥ narma yutaiḥ mada aṃdhaiḥ yaḥ labdhavān tvām vikṛtam virūpam coraiḥ tapobhiḥ giriśam prasādya teṣām tu vākyāni niśamya tāni vicārya budhyā svayam eva dīnaḥ tān śāṃtayitvā vividhaiḥ vacobhiḥ gataḥ tu araṇyam niśi nirjanam tu varṣa ayutam tatra tapaḥ caṃcāra jajāpa jāpyam pādaḥ āhāra hīnaḥ niyama ūrddha bāhuḥ karttum na śakyam hi sura asuraiḥ yat prajvālya vahnim sma juhoti gātra māṃsam sa raktam khalu varṣa mātram tīkṣṇena śastreṇa nikṛtya dehāt sa maṃtrakam pratyaham eva hutvā snāyu asthi śeṣam kuṇapam tadā asau kṣayam gatam śoṇitam eva sarvam yadā asya māṃsāni na saṃti de prakṣetu kāmaḥ tu hutāśanāya athā amaraiḥ śīghrataram prasāditaḥ vabhūva dhātā nutibhiḥ nuṃtaḥ hi nivārayitvā atha pitāmahaḥ tam hi uvāca tvam ca adya varam vṛṇīṣva yasya āpti kāmaḥ tava sarva loke su durlabham dānava tam gṛhāṇa mem prahṛtam tu rājyam prahlāda mukhyāḥ mama saṃtu bhṛtyāḥ nārāyaṇāt vā ditija indra śatro sarvāt janāt sarva mayāt ca śarvāt śrutvā vacaḥ tasya su dāruṇam tat su śaṃkitaḥ padmabhavaḥ tam āha daitya indra sarvam bhavitā tat etad vināśa hetum ca gṛhāṇa kiṃcid yasmāt na jātaḥ naṃ janiṣyate vā yaḥ na praviṣṭaḥ mukham aṃtakasya atyanta dīrgha khalu jīvitam tu bhavādṛśāḥ sat puruṣāḥ tyajaṃtu aṃdhakaḥ uvāca kāla traye yāḥ ca bhavanti nāryaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ ca tathā kaniṣṭhāḥ tāsām ca madhye khalu ratna bhūtā mama api nityam jananī iva kācid kāyena vācā manasā api agamyā nārī nṛ lokasya ca durlabhāya tām kāma yānasya mama astu nā aṃśaḥ daitya indra bhāvāt bhagavān svayaṃbhūḥ vākyam tat ākarṇya sa padmayoniḥ su vismitaḥ śaṃkara pāda padmam sasmāra saṃprāpya nideśa mā āśu śaṃbhoḥ tu tam prāha vai śrutvā tat etat vacanam muni īśa vidhātuḥ āśu praṇipatya bhaktyā lokeśvaram hāṭaka netra putraḥ snāyu asthi śeṣaḥ tu tam āha devam aṃdhakaḥ uvāca snāyu asthi śeṣam kuru māṃsa puṣṭam kareṇa puṇye na ca mām spṛśa adya saṃspṛṣṭa mātraḥ sa ca daitya rājaḥ sampūrṇa dehaḥ balavān babhūva saṃjāta netraḥ subhagaḥ babhūva hṛṣṭaḥ svam evam nagaram viveśa utsṛjya rājyam sakalam ca tasmai prahlāda mukhyāḥ tu atha dānava indrāḥ tam āgatam labdha varam ca matvā bhṛtyāḥ babhūvuḥ vaśa gāḥ tu tasya nāgān suparṇān vara rākṣasān ca gaṃdharva yakṣān mānuṣān tu giri indra vṛkṣān samareṣu sarvān catuṣpadaḥ siṃha mukhān vijigye tatas nukūlāni su darśanāni nārī sahasrāṇi bahūni gatvā rasātale ca eva tathā dharāyām triviṣṭape yāḥ pramadāḥ su rūpāḥ tābhiḥ yutaḥ anyeṣu sa parva teṣu rarāṃma ramyeṣu nadī taṭeṣu krīḍāyamānaḥ sa tu madhya vartī tāsām praharṣāt atha dānava indraḥ tat pīta śiṣṭāni piban pravṛttyai divyāni anyāni divyāni tu phalāni mūlāni su gaṃdhavaṃti saṃprāpya yānāni su vāhanāni mayena sṛṣṭāni gṛha uttamāni puṣpa ardha dhūpa anna vilepanaiḥ ca su śobhitāni adbhuta darśanaiḥ ca saṃkrīḍamānasya gatāni tasya varṣa ayutāni iha sadā andhakaḥ daitya varaḥ sa mūṃḍha om mada aṃdha buddhiḥ kṛta duṣṭa saṃga vedān dvijān vitta mada abhibhūtaḥ na manyate sma api amarān gurūn ca aneka saṃkhyāḥ api varṣa koṭyaḥ praharṣitaḥ maṃdara parvatam tu svarṇa upamām tatra nirīkṣya śobhām babhrāma sainyaiḥ saha māna mattaḥ krīḍā artham āsādya ca tam giri indram matim sa vāsāya cakāra mohāt śubham dṛḍham tatra puram sa kṛtvā mudā āsthitaḥ daitya patiḥ prabhāvāt niveśayāmāsa punar krameṇa atyadbhutam mandara śaila sānau te vai kadācid dvirisusthale hi nārīm surūpām dadṛśuḥ trayaḥ api te śīghra gāḥ daitya varāḥ tu harṣāt dutam mahā daitya patim sametya ūcuḥ yathādṛṣṭam atīva prītyā tathā andhakam vīra varam hi sarve maṃtriṇaḥ ūcuḥ guhā antare dhyāna nimīlita akṣaḥ daitya indra kaśvid muniḥ atra dṛṣṭaḥ rūpa anvitaḥ candra kalā arddhacūḍaḥ kaṭi sthale baddha gaja indra kṛttiḥ gātraḥ kapāla mālā ābharaṇaḥ jaṭālaḥ sa śūla hastaḥ śara tūṇa dhārī mahā dhanuṣmān vivṛta akṣasūtraḥ khaṅgī triśūlī lakuṭī kapadī catur bhujaḥ gauratara ākṛtiḥ hi tasya avidūre puruṣaḥ ca dṛṣṭaḥ sa vānaraḥ ghora mukhaḥ karālaḥ sarva āyudhaḥ rūkṣa karaḥ ca rakṣan sthiraḥ jarat go vṛṣabhaḥ ca śuklaḥ tasya upaviṣṭasya tapasvinaḥ upi su cāru rūpā taruṇī manojñā nārī śubhā pārśva gatā hi tasya dṛṣṭvā ca kācid bhuvi ratna bhūtā pravāla muktāmaṇi hema ratna vastra āvṛtām mālya śubha upagūḍhā sā yena dṛṣṭvā sa ca dṛṣṭimān syāta dṛṣṭena ca anyena kim atra kāryam mānyā maheśasya ca divya nārī bhāryyām muneḥ puṇyavataḥ priyā sā śrutvā iti teṣām vacanāni tāni kāma āturaḥ ghūrṇita sarva gātraḥ visarjayāmāsa muneḥ sakāśam duryodhana ādīn sahasā sa daityaḥ āsādya te tam munim aprameyam vṛhat ratam maṃtri varāḥ hi tasya su rāja nīti pra vaṇāḥ muni īśa praṇamya tam daitya nideśam āduḥ maṃtriṇaḥ ūcuḥ trailokya nāthaḥ bhava kṛt nideśāt iha upaviṣṭaḥ adya vihāra śālī tad maṃtriṇaḥ vai vayam aṃga vīrāḥ tava upakaṃṭham ca samāgatāḥ sma tad preṣitāḥ tvām yat uvāca tat vai śṛṇuṣva saṃdatta manāḥ tapasvin kasya iyam īdṛś taruṇī surūpā deyā śubhā daitya pateḥ muni indra kva idam śarīram tava bhasma digdham kapāla mālā ābharaṇam virūpam tūṇīra sat kārmuka vāṇa khaṅga bhuśuṃḍi śūlā śani tomarāṇi ka jāhnavī puṇyatamā jaṭāye kva ayam śaśī vā kuṇapa asthi khaṇḍam viṣa anala om dīrgha mukhaḥ kva sarpaḥ kva saṃgamaḥ pīna payodharāyāḥ jaradgava ārohaṇam apraśastam kṣamāvataḥ tasya na darśanam ca prayaccha nārīm mama sāntva pūrva striyā tapaḥ kim kuruṣe vimūḍha vimuṃca śastrāṇi mayā adya ca uktaḥ kuruṣva paścā tapaḥ eva śuddham ullaṃghya mad śāsanam apradhṛṣyam vimokṣyase sarvam idam śarīram satu adhikam duṣṭamatim pradhānaḥ maheśvaraḥ laukika bhāva śīlaḥ śivaḥ uvāca śṛṇu prabhāvam mama daitya nātha nyāyyam na vaktum vacanam tvayā evam na aham kvacid svam pitaram smarāmi guhā antare ghoram ananya cīrṇam bhāryā mama iyam taruṇī surūpā etāvat uktvā virarāma śaṃbhuḥ tapasvi veṣaḥ puratas tu teṣām gaṃbhīram etat vacanam niśamya te dānavāḥ tam praṇipatya mūrdhnā babhāṣire daitya patim pramattam praṇamya rājānam adīna sattvāḥ maṃtriṇaḥ ūcuḥ niśācaraḥ caṃcala śaurya dhairyaḥ kva dānavaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ sattva hīnaḥ krūraḥ kṛtaghnaḥ ca sadā eva pāpī kim dānavaḥ sūryasutāt vibheti matvā sva buddhayām tṛṇa vat trilokam mahā ojasā vīra vareṇa nūnam kā aham ca śastrāṇi ca dāruṇāni saṃtrāsa karam yuddham kka vīrakaḥ vānara vakra tulyaḥ niśācaraḥ jarasā jarjara aṃgaḥ kāyam sva rūpaḥ kva ca maṃdabhāgyaḥ balam tvadīyam kva ca vīrudhaḥ vā vajra aśane stulyam iha asti śastram bhavādṛśām nāśa karam ca ghoram maṃtriṇaḥ ūcuḥ iti eva mādīni vacāṃsi bhadram tapasvinā uktāni ca dānava īśa tatas sa teṣām vacanam niśamya jajvāla roṣeṇa sa maṃda buddhiḥ ājya avasiktaḥ tu iva kṛṣṇavarmā satyam hitam tat kuṭilam su tīkṣṇam gṛhīta khaṅgaḥ vara dāna mattaḥ pracaṃḍa vāta anukṛtim ca kurvan iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudra saṃhitāyām pañcame yuddhakher̤e aṃdhakagāṇatatyalābhopākhyāne dūtasaṃvādaḥ nāma catuścatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ gataḥ tatas matta gaja indra gāmī pītvā surām ghūrṇita locanaḥ ca mahā anubhāvaḥ bahu sainya yuktaḥ pracaṃḍa vīraḥ vara vīra yāyī snigdham yathā vīkṣya pataṃgu saṃjñaḥ ca kṛmiḥ div upetya tathā pradaśya āśu punar punar ca saṃpīḍyamānā api sa vīrakeṇa babhūva kāma agni dagdha deha oṃdhakaḥ mahā daitya patiḥ sa mūḍhaḥ pāṣāṇa vṛkṣa aśani toya vahni bhujaṃga śastra astra vibhīṣikābhiḥ saṃpīḍitaḥ asau na punar prapīḍyaḥ pṛṣṭa śva kaḥ tvam samupāgatā usi niśamya tadām sva matam sa tasmai cakāra yuddham sa tu vīrakeṇa tatas tu saṃgrāma śiraḥ vihāya kṣudh kṣāma kaṃṭhaḥ tṛṣitaḥ gataḥ abhūt cūrṇīkṛte khaḍga vare ca khinne palāyamānaḥ smayaḥ saḥ cakruḥ tadā ājim saha vīrakeṇa prahlāda mukhyāḥ ditija pradhānāḥ lajjā aṃkuśa ākṛṣṭa dhiyaḥ babhruvuḥ su dāruṇāḥ śastra śataiḥ anekaiḥ śi canaḥ tatra cakāra yuddham bālaḥ ca bāṇaḥ ca sahasrabāhuḥ bhajiḥ kujaṃbhaḥ tu atha śaṃbaraḥ ca vṛtra ādayaḥ ca api atha vīryavaṃtaḥ te vijitāḥ samam tādvidhākṛtāḥ vai gaṇa vīrakeṇa śeṣe hatānām bahu dānavānām uktam jayati eva hi siddha saṃdhaiḥ medaḥ vasā māṃsa supūya madhye priye mayā yat kṛta pūrvam āsīt dākṣā yaṇīm prāha su sāṃtvayitvā śivaḥ uvāca tasmāt valam yat mama tat praṇaṣṭam matyaiḥ amartyasya yatas prapātaḥ puṇya kṣaya ahī grahaḥ eva jātaḥ divāniśam devi tava prasaṃgāt etāvat uktvā vacanam mahātmā utpādya ghoṣam śanakais cakāra hi śakyam tu sura asuraiḥ yat na tādṛśam varṣa sahasra mātram sā pārvatī maṃdara parvata sthā pratīkṣyamāṇa āgamanam bhavasya pativratā śīla guṇa upapannā ekākinī nityam atho bibhītā guhā aṃtare duḥkha parā vabhūva saṃrakṣitā sā muta vīrakeṇa rātriṃdivam paṃca śatāni paṃca kruddhaḥ sa sainyaḥ saha vīrakeṇa khadvaiḥ sa kutaiḥ saha bhidipāle gadā bhuśuṃḍībhiḥ atho prakāṃḍaiḥ nārāca mukhyaiḥ niśitaiḥ ca śūlaiḥ paraśvadhaiḥ tomara mudgaraiḥ ca nadīdhiti bhinna tanuḥ papāta dvāram guhāyā pihitam samastam apāvṛtam kartum aśakyam āsīt nirīkṣya devī ditijān su ghorān devī sakhībhiḥ sahitā ca viṣṇum brahmā tathā saṃsmṛta mātraḥ eva strī rūpa dhārī bhagavān ca viṣṇuḥ sarvaiḥ saha sainyakaiḥ śva strī rūpam āsthāya samāgatāḥ te bhūtvā striyaḥ te viviśuḥ tadānīm muni indra saṃghāḥ ca mahā anubhāvāḥ siddhāḥ ca nāgāḥ tu atha guhyakāḥ ca guhā aṃtaram parvatarāja putryāḥ yasmāt su rājya āsana saṃsthitānām ataḥpure saṃgamanam viruddham tatas sahasrāṇi nitaṃvinīnām ananta saṃkhyāni api darśayatyaḥ rūpāṇi divyāni mahā adbhutāni gauryai guhāyām tu sa vīrakāyai striyaḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ girirāja kanyām guhā aṃtaram parvatarāja putryā strībhiḥ sahasraiḥ ca śataiḥ kalpa antara megha ghoṣāḥ bheryaḥ ca saṃgrāma jaya pradāḥ tu ghmātāḥ su śaṃkhāḥ mvinībhiḥ mūrchāḥ vihāya adbhuta caṃḍa vīryaḥ sa vīrakaḥ vai puratas sthitaḥ tu pragṛhya śastrāṇi mahā rathānām taiḥ eva śastraiḥ ditijam jaghāna brāhmī tatas daṃḍakarā viruddhā gaurī tadā krodha parīta cetāḥ viniryayaḥ lāṃgala daṇḍa hastā vyoma alakā kāṃcana tulya varṇā dhārā sahasra ākulam ugra vegam baiḍaujasī vajra karā tadānīm vaiśvānarī śaktiḥ asaumya vakrā yāmyā ca su tīkṣṇa khaṅga udyata pāṇi rūpā samāyayau nairṛti ghora cāpā toyālikā vāraṇa pāśa hastā vinirgatā yuddham abhīpsamānā pracaṃḍa vāta prabhavā ca devīm kṣudhā vapuḥ tvam kuśa pāṇiḥ eva kalpa anta vahni pratimām gadā ca pāṇau gṛhī tvā dhanada udbhavā ca yājñeśvarī tīkṣṇa mukhā virūpā nakha āyudhām nāga bhayaṃkarī ca etāḥ tathā anyāḥ śataśas hi devyaḥ su nirgatāḥ saṃkula yuddha bhūmim dṛṣṭvā ca tat sainyam anaṃta pāram vivarṇa varṇāḥ ca su vismitāḥ ca samākulāḥ saṃcakitāḥ bhayāt vai devyaḥ babhūvuḥ hṛd adīna sattvāḥ su saṃgata tvena girīśa putryāḥ senāpatiḥ vīra su ghora vīryaḥ cakruḥ mahā yuddham abhūta pūrva nidhāya buddhau ditijāḥ pradhānāḥ nivartanam mṛtyum atha ātmanaḥ ca nārībhiḥ anye vara dāna sattvāḥ ati adbhutam tatra cakāra yuddham gaurī tadānīm sahitā sakhībhiḥ kṛtvā raṇe senāpatim vīraka ghora vīryam hiraṇyanetra ātmajaḥ eva bhūpaḥ ca mahā vyūham aram sukarmā saṃbhāvya viṣṇum ca nirīkṣya yāmyām su dāruṇam mukham karālam vidhi sevayā asya tasmin kṛte bhagavān ājagāma kalpa anta ghora arka sahasra kānti kīrṇan ca vai kupitaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ cakruḥ mahā yuddham atīva mātram nāryaḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ saha vīrakeṇa praṇamya gaurī giriśam ca mūṃdarbhā saṃdarśayan bhartuḥ atīva śauryyam gaurī prayuddham ca cakāra hṛṣṭā haraḥ tatas parvatarāja putrīm kaṃṭhe gṛhītvā tu guhām praviṣṭaḥ ramā sahasrāṇi visarjitāni gaurī ca sanmāna śataiḥ prapūjya guhā mukhe vīrakam eva sthāpayan naḥ tatas na gaurīm giriśam ca dṛṣṭvā asura īśvaraḥ nīti vicakṣaṇaḥ hi drutam sva dūtam vidha sāṃkhyam eva sa preṣayāmāsa śiva upakaṃṭham jagāda vākyam tu sa garva mugram praviśya śaṃbhum praṇipatya mūrdhnā dūtaḥ uvāca viviśe guhām tu hi eṣaḥ andhaka rasvām samuvāca vākyam na aya na kārya tava kiṃcid asti vimuṃca nārīm taruṇīm surūpām prāyas bhavān tāpasaḥ tat juṣasva kṣātam mayā yat kamanīyam antar nayāmi pātāla tala anurūpam yama kṣayam tāpasa dhūrtta hi tvām etat vacaḥ dūta mukhāt niśamya kapālamālī tam uvāca kopāt jvalan viṣādena mahān trinetraḥ satām gatiḥ duṣṭa śivaḥ uvāca āyāntu daityāḥ ca balena mattāḥ vicārya evam tu kṛtam mayā etat śarīra yātrā api kutas tu aśakteḥ kurvantu yat yat hitam tu teṣām etat vacaḥ tat vigaśaḥ api tasmāt śrutvā harāt vinirgatam eva hṛṣṭaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe yuddhaprārambhabhūtasaṃvādavarṇanam nāma pañcacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ tasya uṅgita jñaḥ ca sa daitya rājaḥ gadām gṛhītvā tvaritaḥ sa sainyaḥ kṛtvā atha sa aṅge gila nāmadheyam su dāruṇam deva vairaiḥ abhedyam anye tatas vīrakam eva śastraiḥ avākiran chela sutām tathā anye dvāram hi kecid vicairam babhañjuḥ puṣpāṇi patrāṇi vināśayeyuḥ phalāni mūlāni jalam ca hṛdyam udyāna mārgān api khaṇḍayeyuḥ viloḍayeyuḥ muditāḥ ca kecid śṛṅgāṇi śailasya ca bhānumanti tatas haraḥ saṃsmṛtavān sva sainyam samāhvayan kupitaḥ śūlapāṇiḥ bhūtāni ca anyāni su dāruṇāni devān sa sainyān saha viṣṇu mukhyān āhūta mātra anugaṇāḥ sa sainyāḥ rathaiḥ gajaiḥ vāji vṛṣaiḥ ca gobhiḥ uṣṭraiḥ kharaiḥ pakṣi varaiḥ ca siṃhaiḥ te sarve devāḥ saha bhūta saṃghaiḥ vyāghraiḥ mṛgaiḥ sūkara sārasaiḥ ca sa mīna matsyaiḥ śiśumāra mukhyaiḥ anyaiḥ ca nānāvidha jīva saṃghaiḥ viśīrṇa veśāḥ bhujaṃgamaiḥ preta śataiḥ piśācaiḥ divyaiḥ vimānaiḥ kamala ākaraiḥ ca nadīnadaiḥ parvata vāhanaiḥ ca samāgatāḥ prāñjalayaḥ praṇamya kapardinam tasthuḥ adīna sattvāḥ senāpatim vīrakam eva kṛtvā visṛjayāmāsa raṇāya devān viśrānta vāhān atha tān pināki yuddhe sthiram labdha jayam pradhānam saṃpreṣitāḥ te tu maheśvareṇa cakṣuḥ yuga anta pratimam ca yuddham maryāda hīnam sāgilena sarvaiḥ daitya indra sainyena sadā eva ghoram krodhāt nigīrṇāḥ tridaśāḥ tu saṃkhyāḥ tasmin kṣaṇe yudhyamānāḥ ca sarve brahma indra viṣṇu arka śaśāṅka mukhyāḥ vihāya saṃgrāma śiraḥ guhām tām praviśya śarvam praṇipatya mūrdhnā provāca duḥkha abhihataḥ smarārim suvīrakaḥ vāgamiravaḥ atha vṛttam ajeyam tvām prāptaḥ pratibhaya manāḥ mārutagatiḥ svayam viṣṇuḥ devaḥ kanakakaśipum kaśyapa sutam tatas teṣām vākyāt pratidinam asau daitya gilanaḥ kṣudhā ārtaḥ saṃgrāmāt dhamati punar āmoda muditaḥ hatān devaiḥ daityān punar amṛta vidyā stuti padaiḥ sa vīryān saṃhṛṣṭa antraṇa śata viyuktān prakurute varam prāṇāḥ tyājyāḥ tava mama tu saṃgrāma samaye bhavān sākṣī bhūtaḥ kṣaṇam api vṛtaḥ kārya karaṇe pragāyat sā mā ākhyam dinakara kara ākāra vapuṣā prahāsāt tad nānnām tadanum nihatam tena ca tamaḥ śilā cūrṇe bhuktvā pravara muninā yaḥ tu janitaḥ sa kṛtvā saṃgrāmam puram api purā yaḥ ca jitavān mahārudraḥ sadyas sa khalu diti jena ati gilitaḥ tatas ca asau nandī niśita śara śūla asi sahitaḥ pradhānaḥ yodhānām muni vara śatānām api mahān nivāsaḥ vidyānām śama dama mahā dhairya sa hitaḥ nirīkṣya evam paśvāt vṛṣabha varam āruhya bhagavān kapardī yuddha arthī vighasa ditijam sammukha mukhaḥ samudgīrṇāḥ sarve kamala ja balāri indu dinapāḥ prahṛṣṭam tat sainyam punar api mahā yuddham akarot tadā baddha ānītaḥ paśuḥ iva gaṇaiḥ bhūtapataye nirgīrṇaḥ tena asau tripura ripuṇā a dānava guruḥ pramattā eva etāḥ su dṛḍha kara tuḍaiḥ api khagaiḥ vṛkaiḥ nānā vikīrṇa saṃgrāme kanakakaśipoḥ vaṃśa jana kaśviram yuddham kṛtvā hari hara mahā indraiḥ ca vijitaḥ praviṣṭe pātāle giri jaladhi raṃbhrāṇi api tathā tatas sainye kṣīṇe ditija vṛṣabha śva aṃdhaka varaḥ pīḍita tanuḥ tathā vṛkṣaiḥ sapaiḥ aśani nivahaiḥ śastra paṭṭalaiḥ virūpaiḥ māyābhiḥ kapaṭa racanā śambara śataiḥ tadīyāt viṣyandāt kṣiti tala gataiḥ andhaka gaṇaiḥ ati vyāptam ghoram vikṛ vadanam sva ātma sadṛśam tadā gāt pramatha patim āhūya matimān cakāra uyam rūpam vikṛta vadanam straiṇam ajitam karālam saṃśuṣkam bahu bhuja latā ākrāṃta kupitaḥ viniṣkrāṃtaḥ karṇāt guṇa śirasi śaṃbhoḥ ca bhagavān giriśam smaran kṣātram dharmam sva kula vihitam śāśvatam ajam raṇe śāṃtaḥ paścāt pramatha patinā bhinna hṛdaya triśūle saṃprotaḥ nabhasi vidhṛtaḥ sthāṇu sadṛśaḥ adhas kāyaḥ śuṣkaḥ stapana kiraṇaiḥ jīrṇa tanumāḥ alāsāraiḥ medhaiḥ pavana sahitaiḥ kvedita vapuḥ viśīrṇaḥ tigmāṃśoḥ tuhina śa kalā ākāra śakalaḥ tathābhūtaḥ prāṇān tat api na jahau daitya vṛṣabhaḥ tadā tuṣṭaḥ śaṃbhuḥ parama karuṇā vāriṃdhiḥ asau dadau tasmai prītyā gaṇapati padam tena vinutaḥ hari brahma ādyāḥ te parama nutibhiḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ alam nata skaṃdhāḥ prītāḥ jaya jaya giram procya sukhitāḥ cakāra ājñām krīḍām giri vara sutām prāpya muditām tathā putram ghorāt vighasa vadanāt muktam anagham iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe aṃdhakavadhopākhyāne aṃdhakayuddhavarṇanam nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca iti śrutam samāsāt me tat punar brūhi vistarāt kim cakāra mahā yogī jaṭhara sthaḥ pinākinaḥ na dadāha katham śaṃbhoḥ śukram tam jaṭhara analaḥ kalpa anta dahanaḥ kālaḥ dīpta tejāḥ ca bhārgavaḥ viniṣkrāṃtaḥ katham dhīmān śaṃbhoḥ jaṭhara paṃjarāt katham ārādhayāmāsa kiyatkālam sa bhārgavaḥ atha ca labdhavān vidyām tām mṛtyu śamanīm parām kā sā vidyā parā tāta yayā lebha indhakaḥ gāṇapatyam katham śūlāt vinirgataḥ devadevasya vai śaṃbhoḥ muneḥ līlā vihāriṇaḥ etat sarvam aśeṣeṇa mahā dhī mana kṛpāṃḥ kuru śiva līlā amṛtam tāta śṛṇvataḥ kathayasva me pravartamāne samare śaṃkara aṃghakayoḥ tayoḥ purā jayaḥ babhūva api daityānām bala śāli nām śiva prabhāvāt abhavat pramathānām mune jayaḥ tat śrutvā āsīt viṣaṇṇaḥ hi mahā daitya uṃdhaka asuraḥ babhāṣa idam vi cārya atha sa aṃjaliḥ nīti vittamaḥ aṃdhakaḥ uvāca parājitāḥ bhavāmaḥ naḥ sarvadā jaya śālinaḥ tvad prabhāvāt sadā devān samastān sa anugān vayam gajāḥ iva haribhyaḥ ca tākṣryebhyaḥ iva pannagāḥ anirbhedyam pavi vyūham viviśuḥ daitya dānavāḥ sthitvā carāmaḥ niśśaṃkam ājau api hi bhārgava asurān śatrubhiḥ vīraiḥ arditān ca mṛtān api pramathaiḥ bhīma vikrāṃtaiḥ krāṃtān mṛtyu pramāthibhiḥ sūditān pa titān paśya huṃḍāṃdīn mad guṇān varān pramathāḥ asurān sarvān kṛpayā jīvayiṣyataḥ ittham andhaka vākyam sa śrutvā dhīraḥ hi bhārgavaḥ kim kartavyam mayā adya api dakṣemam me syāt katham tu iti vidheyam śaṃkarāt prāptā tad guṇān prati yojaye tad vaṇe marditān vīraiḥ pramathaiḥ śaṃkara anugaiḥ vicārya śukreṇa dhiyā tad vāṇī svīkṛtā tadā bhārgavaḥ śiva pāda abjam smṛtvā svasthe na cetasā śukraḥ uvāca dussaham kaṇadhūmam vai pītvā varṣa sahasrakam vidyā iyam īśvarāt prāptā baṃdhūnām sukha dā sadā utthāpayiṣye mlānāni śasyāni jala muc yathā muhūrte ismin sa draṣṭāsi daityān tān utthitān nijān iti uktvā saḥ uṃdhakam śukraḥ vidyām āvartayat kaviḥ ekaikam daityam uddiśya smṛtvā vidyeśam ādarāt vidyā vartana mātreṇa te sarve daitya dānavāḥ uttasthuḥ yugapad vīrāḥ suptāḥ iva dhṛta āyudhāḥ sadā abhyastāḥ yathā vedāḥ samare vā yathā ambudāḥ śraddhayā arthāḥ tathā dattāḥ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ yathā āpadi raṇa udyatāḥ punar ca āsan garjataḥ vikaṭān ravān śukreṇa ujjīvitān dṛṣṭvā pramathāḥ daitya dānavān vijñāpyam evam karma etat eva īśe śaṃkare akhilam śivaḥ uvāca a kitvam ārṣam vyāsaḥ uvāca śukre nigīrṇe rudreṇa kim akārṣuḥ ca dānavāḥ aṃdhaka īśāḥ mahā vīrāḥ vada tattvam mahā mune śiraḥ vid dīnāḥ iva deha saṃghāḥ dvijāḥ yathā ca adhyayanena hīnāḥ yathā viśūrāḥ khalu kṣatriyāḥ ca satyam vinā dharma gaṇaḥ yathā eva daityān tu huḍahuṃḍa ādīn mahā dhīra parākramaḥ aṃdhakaḥ uvāca vīraiḥ taiḥ smṛtvā guru pada aṃbujam guroḥ kāvyasya sukha da smṛtvā caraṇa paṃkajau yāṃti te yāṃti niyatam aṃdhatāmisram ālayam ayaśaḥ tamasā khyātim malinīkṛtya bhūriśas kim dānaiḥ kim tapobhiḥ ca kim tīrthaṃparimajjanaiḥ dhārātīrthe yadi snānam punarbhava mala apahe saṃpradhārya iti tat vākyam daityāḥ te danujāḥ tathā ati svanaiḥ avāpūri dyāvābhūmyoḥ yat aṃtaram atha te pramathāḥ vīrāḥ naṃdi prabhṛtayaḥ tadā dṛṣṭvā sainyam bhajyamānam aṃdhakena balīyasā skaṃdaḥ vināyakaḥ naṃdī somanaṃdi ādayaḥ pare pramathā prabalā bīsa śśaṃkarasya gaṇām nijāḥ vināyakena skaṃdena naṃdinā soma naṃdinā ityādyaiḥ tu kaḥ api aṃdhakīkṛtaḥ sam varṣāṇām śatam kukṣau bhavasya paritas bhraman imam maṃtra varam śaṃbhoḥ jaṭhara paṃjarāt niṣkrāntaḥ liṃga mārgeṇa praṇanāma tatas śivam gauryā gṛhītaḥ putra artham tad avighna īśvarīkṛtaḥ atha kāvyam viniṣkānta śukra mārgeṇa bhārgavam dṛṣṭvā uvāca maheśānī om vihasya karuṇā nidhiḥ nāma śivam bhūyas tuṣṭāva vihita aṃjaliḥ śukraḥ uvāca ātmanepadam ārṣam tvam aṣṭamūrttiḥ tvam ananta mūrtiḥ tvam iṣṭa daḥ sarvam sura asurāṇām aniṣṭa dṛṣṭaḥ ca vimardaka śva stoṣye ha nṛtyam katham īdṛśam tvām iti stutvā śivam śukraḥ punar natvā śiva ājñayā nigīrṇanam iti proktam śaṃkareṇa kaveḥ raṇe śṛṇu maṃtram ca tam jeptaḥ yaḥ śaṃbhoḥ kavinā udare iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe śukranigīrṇanam nāma aṣṭacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ om namaḥ te deveśāya sura asura namaskṛtāya bhūta bha vya mahādevāya harita piṃgala locanāya balāya buddhi rūpiṇe vaiyāghra vasanacchadāya araṇeyāya trailokya prabhave īśvarāya harāya harita netrāya yugānta karaṇāya nalāya gaṇeśāya lokapālāya mahābhujāya mahāhastāya śūline mahā daṃṣṭriṇe kālāya maheśvarāya avyayāya kāla rūpiṇe nīlagrīvāya mahodarāya gaṇāḥ dhyakṣāya sarvātmane sarvaṃbhāvanāya sarvagāya mṛtyu haṃtre brahma ha imam mantra varam japtvā śukraḥ jaṭhara paṃjarāt niṣkrāntaḥ liṃga mārgeṇa śaṃbhoḥ śrukram iva utka gauryā gṛhītaḥ putra artham viśveśena tatas kṛtaḥ mahe śvarāt punar jātaḥ śukraḥ vedanidhiḥ muniḥ dadarśa śūle saṃśuṣkam dhyāyatam parameśvaram aṃdhakam dhairya saṃpannam dānava īśam tapasvinam mahādevam virū iḍabhāvaḥ ārṣaḥ amṛtam śāśvatam sthāṇum nīlakaṃṭham pinākinam vṛṣabha akṣam mahā jñeyam puruṣam sarva kāma dam virūpam giriśam bhīmam sṛkkiṇam rakta vāsasam gūḍha vratam gupta maṃtram gaṃbhīram caram vīram vīra haṇam ghoram virūpam māṃsalam paṭum mahā māṃsa ādam unmattam bhairavam vai maheśvaram trailokya drāvaṇam lubdham lubdhakam yajñasūdanam kṛttikānām sutaiḥ yuktam unmattam kṛttivāsasam gaja kṛtti parīdhānam kṣubdham bhujaga bhūṣaṇam datta ālaṃbam ca vetālam ghoram śākini pūjitam bhasma aṃgam jaṭilam śuddham bheruṃḍa śata sevitam bhūteśvaram bhūta nātham paṃcabhūta āśritam khagam krodhitam niṣṭharam caṇḍam caṃḍīśam caṇḍikāpriyam caṇḍam tuṃgam garutmaṃtam nityam āsava bhojanam lelihānam mahā raudram mṛtyum mṛtyoḥ agocaram mṛtyoḥ mṛtyum mahāsenam śmaśāna araṇya vāsinam rāgam virāgam rāga aṃdham vīta rāga śata ācitam arddhanārīśvaram bhānum bhānu koṭi śata prabham yajñam yajñapatim rudram īśānam vara dam śivam divyena amṛta varṣeṇa saḥ abhiṣiktaḥ kaparddinā tuṣṭena mocitam tasmāt śūla agrāt avaropitaḥ uktaḥ ca atha mahā daityaḥ maheśānena saḥ aghakaḥ asuraḥ sāṃtva pūrvam yat kṛtam sarvam mahātmanā īśvaraḥ uvāca ārādhitaḥ tvayā nityam sarva nirdhūta kalmaṣaḥ prāṇa saṃdhāraṇāt asti yat ca puṇya phalam tava bhūmau jānu drayam kṛtvā bhagavaṃtam umāpatim aṃdhakaḥ uvāca harṣa gadgadayā vācā mayā pūrvam raṇa ajire a jānatā tvām tat sarvam prabho manasi mā kṛthāḥ pārvatyām api duṣṭam yat kāma doṣāt kṛtam mayā kṣamyatām me mahādeva kṛpaṇaḥ duḥkhitaḥ bhṛśam duḥkhitasya dayā kāryā kṛpaṇasya viśeṣataḥ saḥ ham dīnaḥ bhakti yuktaḥ āgataḥ śaraṇam tava rakṣā mayi vidhātavyā racitaḥ ayam mayā aṃjaliḥ iyam devī jaganmātā parituṣṭā mama upari krodham vihāya sakalam prasannā mām nirīkṣatām kā asyāḥ krodhaḥ tat soḍhā na aham addhā inducūḍa śaṃbho maheśvaram kka bhavān parama udāraḥ kva ca aham vivaśīkṛtaḥ kāma krodha ādibhiḥ doṣaḥ rjarasā mṛtyunā tathā ayam te vīrakaḥ putraḥ yuddha śauṃḍaḥ mahā balaḥ kṛpaṇam mām samālakṣya mā manyu vaśam anvagāḥ tuṣāra hāra śītāṃśu śaṃkha kunde indu varṇa bhāj paśya imām pārvatīm nityam mātaram guru gauravāt nivaseyam gaṇaiḥ sārddham śāṃta ātmā yoga ciṃtakaḥ ghyāyan trilocanam devam pārvatīm prekṣya mātaram tatas dṛṣṭaḥ tu rudreṇa prasannena eva cakṣuṣā smṛtavān pūrva vṛttāṃtam ātma naḥ janma ca adbhutam tasmin smṛte ca vṛttāṃte tatas pūrṇa manorathaḥ praṇamya mātā pitarā kṛtakṛtyaḥ abhavat tatas pārvatyā sūryu pātrāt śśaṃkareṇa ca dhīmatā tathā abhilaṣitam lebhe tuṣṭāt vāla induśekharāt gāṇapatyam mahādeva prasādāt para asau khya dam mṛtyuṃjayaḥ ca kathitaḥ maṃtraḥ mṛtyu vināśanaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe aṃdhakagaṇajīvitaprāptivarṇanam nāma ikonapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śṛṇu vyāsa yathā prāptā mṛtyu praśamanī parā vidyā kāvyena suninā śivāt mṛtyuñjaya abhidhāt sthāpayāmāsa tatra eva liṃgam śaṃbhoḥ parātmanaḥ snāpa yāmāsa deveśam su gaṃdha snapanaiḥ vahu mallikā śatapatrībhiḥ iṃsa dhuvāraiḥ sa kiṃśukeḥ bandhūka puṣpaiḥ punnāgaiḥ nāga keśara keśaraiḥ sa avadhānena su prītyā sa samānace śaṃkaram nānā prakāram vidhinā maheśam sa samarca pati antā iti ya ga ārṣaḥ prakṣālya cetasaḥ atyantam cāṃcalya ākhyam mahā malam kāvyam ittham tapaḥ ghoram kurvantam dṛḍha mānasam prasasāda sa tam vīkṣya bhārgavāya maheśvaraḥ tapasā anena te nityam prasannaḥ aham viśeṣataḥ prītyā dāsye akhilān kāmān na ādeyam vidyate tava niśamya iti vacaḥ śaṃbho mahāsu kha karam varam praṇanāma muddā śaṃbhum aṃbhoja nayanaḥ dvijaḥ bhārgavaḥ uvāca tvam bhābhiḥ ābhiḥ abhibhūya tamaḥ samastam astam nayasi bhimata anim niśācarāṇām himāṃśo pīyūṣa pūra paripūrita tat namaḥ te tvam pāvane pathi sadā gatiḥ api upāsyaḥ kaḥ tvām vinā bhuvana jīvana jīvati iha viśvaka pāvaka natāvaka pāvaka eka śakte ṛte mṛtavatā amṛta divya kārya jagat aho jagat antarātman tvam pāvakaḥ pratipadam śama daḥ namaḥ te na iha īśvara viśvam etat mahādevaḥ uvāca liṃga sthāpana puṇyena liṃgasya ārā dhanena ca ye arcayiṣyanti manujāḥ teṣām siddhiḥ bhaviṣyati bhārgavaḥ api nijam dhāma prāpa saṃtuṣṭa mānasaḥ iti te kathitam vyāsa yathā prāptā tapaḥ balāt iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṇḍe mṛtasaṃjīvinī vidyāprāptivarṇanam nāma pañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca sanatkumāra sarvajña śrāvitā su kathā adbhutā bhavatā anugrahāt prītyā śaṃbhu anugraha nirbharā gāṇapatyam dadau prītyā yathā bāṇa asurāya vai śṛṇu kathām śaṃbhoḥ parātmanaḥ gāṇapatyam yathā prītyā dadau bāmmāsurāya hi kṛṣṇena samaraḥ upi atra śaṃbhoḥ bāṇa anugṛhṇataḥ atra anurūpam śṛṇu me śiva līlā anvitam param itihāsam mahā puṇyam manaḥ śrotra sukha āvaham brahma putra marīci yoṃ suniḥ āsīt mahāmatiḥ sṛṣṭi pravṛddhakaḥ a liṭ ārṣaḥ tasya trayodaśa mitāḥ dakṣa kanyāḥ suśīlīkāḥ kaśyapasya muneḥ vyāsa patnyāḥ ca āsan patitratāḥ tatra jyeṣṭā ditiśvāsī daityāḥ tad tanayāḥ smṛtāḥ anyāsām ca sutāḥ jātāḥ deva ādyāḥ sa carācarāḥ jyeṣṭhāyāḥ prathamau putrau diteḥ ca āstām mahā balau hrāda anuhlāda saṃhrāda prahlāda aśva iti anukramāt prahlādaḥ tatra ahi mahā viṣṇu bhaktaḥ jiṃ ta indriyaḥ yam nā aśitum na śaktāḥ te abhavan detyāḥ ca ke api ha śakrāya sva śiraḥ yaḥ adaḥ āyācamānāya viprataḥ tasya putraḥ baliḥ ca āsīt mahādānī śivapriyaḥ śoṇita ākhye pure sthitvā sa rājyam akarot purā trailokyam ca balāt jitvā tat nāthān asure śvaraḥ tasya bāṇa asurasya iva śiva bhaktasya ca amarāḥ tasya rājye amarān hitvām na abhavan duḥkhitāḥ prajāḥ tāṃḍavena hi nṛtyena atoṣayat tam maheśvaram dadarśa kṛpayā dṛṣṭayā śaṃkaraḥ bhakta vatsalaḥ śaṃkaraḥ uvāca bāleyaḥ sa mahā daityaḥ bāṇaḥ bhakta varaḥ sudhīḥ praṇamya śaṃkaram bhaktyā para bāṇāsuraḥ uvāca bali putraḥ sa vai bāṇaḥ mohitaḥ śiva māyayā sa bhakta vatsalaḥ śaṃbhuḥ da tvā tasmai varān ca tān tatra uvāsa tathā prītyā sa gaṇaḥ sa sutaḥ prabhuḥ nadī tīre haraḥ krīḍām ramye śoṇitaka āhvaye jeyuḥ praṇeyuḥ munayaḥ ānarcuḥ stuṣṭuvuḥ ca tam āyayuḥ siddha saṃghāḥ ca dadṛśuḥ śāṃkarīm ratim kutarkikāḥ vineśuḥ ca mlecchāḥ ca paripaṃthi naḥ mātaraḥ abhimukhāḥ tasthuḥ vineśuḥ ca vibhīṣikāḥ rudra sadbhāva bhaktānām bhava doṣāḥ ca vistṛtāḥ tasmin dṛṣṭe prajāḥ sarvāḥ su prītim paramām yayuḥ vavalguḥ munayaḥ siddhāḥ strīṇām dṛṣṭvā viceṣṭitam pupuṣuḥ śvā api ṛtavaḥ sva prabhāvam tu tatra ca vavuḥ vātāḥ ca mṛdavaḥ puṣpa kesara dhūsarāḥ cukūjuḥ pakṣi ḍhak ārṣaḥ saṃghāḥ ca śokhinām madhu lampaṭāḥ puṣpa bhāra avanaddhānām rāravyeran śva kokilāḥ madhuram kāma jananam vaneṣu upavaneṣu ca tatas krīḍā vihāre tu mattaḥ bāla indu śekharaḥ anirjitena kāmena dṛṣṭaḥ provāca nandinam vā mām ānaya gaurīm tvam kailāsāt kṛ ta maṃḍanām śīghram asmāt vanāt gatvā yuktvā kṛṣṇām iha ānaya su pra namya rahaḥ dūtaḥ śaṃkarasya kṛta aṃjaliḥ naṃdīśvaraḥ uvāca draṣṭum icchati devi tvām devadevaḥ maheśvaraḥ sva vallabhām rūpa kṛtām mayā uktam deśataḥ tatas tad vacanāt vaurī maṃḍanam kartum ādarāt udyatā abhūt muni śreṣṭha pativrata parāyaṇā āgacchāmi prabhum gaccha vada tam tvam mama ājñayā ājagāma tatas naṃdī rudra āsannam manogatiḥ vāḍham uktvā sa tām gatvā gaurīm āha su locanām draṣṭum icchati te bhartā kṛta veṣām manoramām śaṅkaraḥ bahudhā devi vihartum saṃpratīkṣate evam labdha bhāvaḥ yathā sadyas pārvatyāḥ darśana utsukaḥ ayam pinākī kāmāriḥ vṛṇuyāt yām nitaṃbinīm sarvāsām divya nārīṇām rājñī bhavati vai dhruvam vīkṣaṇam gauri rūpeṇa krīḍayet manmathaiḥ gaṇaiḥ kāmaḥ ayam iṃtim kāmārim ūcuḥ anyonyam āhatāḥ spaṣṭam śaknoti yā dākṣāyaṇī striyam sāṃgacche tatra niśśaṃkam mohayet pārvatīpatim kūṣmāṃḍa tanayām tatra śaṃkaram spraṣṭum utsahe aham gaurī su rūpeṇa citralekhā vacaḥ travīt yat adhāt mohinī rūpam keśavaḥ mohana icchayā purā tat vaiṣṇavam yogam āśritya paramārthataḥ urvaśyāḥ ca tatas dṛṣṭvā rūpasya parivartanam kālī rūpam ghṛtācī tu viśvācī cāṃḍikam vapuḥ raṃbhā ca gāyatram menakā tathā sa hajanyā jayā rūpam vaijayam puṃjaka sthalī mātṛṇām api anuktānām anuktāḥ ca apsarovarāḥ yatnāt rūpāṇi anu tatas tāsām tu rūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā kuṃbhāṃḍa naṃdinī vaiṣṇavāt ātma yogāt ca vijñāta arthāḥ vyaḍaṃbayan ūṣā bāṇāsurasutā divya yoṃ ga viśāradā mahā rakta abja saṃkāśam caraṇam ca uktam aprabham divya lakṣaṇa saṃyuktam manaḥ abhīṣṭa artha dāyakam tasyāḥ ramaṇa saṃkalpam vijñāya girijā tatas uvāca sarva vijñānā sarva antaryāminī śivā svaārṣaḥ svarūpam iti śeṣaḥ yatas mama sva rūpam vai dhṛta mūṣe sakhi tvayā asmin tu kārtike māsi ṛtu dharmāḥ tu mādhave dvādaśyām śuka pakṣe tu yaḥ tu ghore niśāṃgame kṛta upavāsīm tvām bhoktā suptām aṃtar pure naraḥ ā bālyāt viṣṇu bhaktā asi yatas aniśa mataṃdvitā atha sā pārvatī devī kṛta kautukamaṃḍanā rudra saṃnidhim āgatya cikrīḍe tena śaṃbhunā tatas rata aṃte bhagavān udraśvā darśanam yayau iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām paṃcame yuddha khaṇḍe ūṣācaritravarṇanam śivaśivāvivāhavarṇanam nāma ekapaṃcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śṛṇuṣva anyat caritram ca śivasya paramātmanaḥ purā bāṇāsuraḥ nāma daiva doṣāt ca garvitaḥ jñātvā saṃtuṣṭa manasam pārvatī vallabham śivam uvāca ca asuroḥ bāṇaḥ nata skandhaḥ kṛta aṃjaliḥ bāṇaḥ uvāca tvad prasādāt valī ca aham śṛṇu meṃ paramam vacaḥ bāhūnām giri tulyānām vinā yuddham vṛṣadhvajam mayā yamaḥ kṛtaḥ yoddhā vahniḥ vai kṛtakaḥ mahān varuṇaḥ ca api gopālaḥ gavām pālayitā tathā gajādhyakṣaḥ kuberaḥ tu sairandhrī ca api nirṛtiḥ jita śva ākhaṇḍalaḥ loke kara dāyī sadā kṛtaḥ śatru hasta prayuktaiḥ śva śastra astreḥ jarjarīkṛtāḥ patantu śatru hastāt vā pātayantu sahasradhā kṛtvā abravīt mahā manyuḥ bhakta bādhā apahārakaḥ darpasya asya praśamanam lapsyase ca āśu dāruṇam tatra te giri saṃkāśāḥ bāhavaḥ anala kāṣṭha vat chinnām bhūmau patiṣyati śastra astraiḥ kadalīkṛtāḥ yat eṣa mānuṣa śiraḥ mayūra sahitaḥ dhvajaḥ vidyate tava duṣṭa ātman tasya syāt patanam yadā sthāpitasya āyudhāgāre vinā vāta kṛtam bhayam tadā yuddham mahā ghoram saṃprāptam iti nidhāya ghoram saṃgrāmam gacchethāḥ sarva sainyavān tathā mahā utpātān tatra dṛṣṭvā asi durmate kuṃbhaṇḍāya yathāvṛttam pṛṣṭaḥ provāca harṣitaḥ paryaikṣiṣṭa asuraḥ bāṇaḥ tam yogam hi utsukaḥ sadā dṛṣṭvā tatra asuraḥ bāṇaḥ hṛṣṭaḥ yuddhāya niryayau sa sva sainyam samāhūya saṃyuktaḥ sa aṣṭabhiḥ gaṇaiḥ iṣṭim sāṃgrāmikām kṛtvā dṛṣṭvā sāṃgrāmikam madhu iti hṛd kamale kṛtvā kaḥ kasmāt āgamiṣyati yoddhā raṇa priyaḥ yastuḥ nānā śastra astra pāragaḥ tathā śastraiḥ mahā tīkṣṇaiḥ china etasmin antare kālaḥ saṃprāptaḥ śaṃkareṇa hi yatra sā bāṇa duhitā sujātā kṛta maṃgalā mādhavam mādhave māsi pūjayitvā mahāniśi suptā ca tiḥpure gupte strī bhāvam upalaṃbhitā gauryā saṃpreṣitena api vyākṛṣṭāḥ divya māyayā kṛṣṇa ātmaja ātmajena atha rudaṃtī sāhi anātha vat sa ca api tām balāt bhukkā pārvatyāḥ sa khibhiḥ punar nītaḥ tu divya yogena dvārakām nimiṣa aṃtarāt mṛditā sā tadā utthāya rudaṃtī vividhāḥ giraḥ sakhībhyaḥ kathayitvā tu dehatyāge kṛtakṣaṇā sakhyā akṛtātmanaḥ doṣam sā vyāsa smāritā punar sarvam tad pūrva vṛttāṃtam tatas dṛṣṭvā ca sā abhavat ūṣā bāṇasya tanayā kuṃbhāṃḍa tanayā mune uṣā uvāca sākha yadi eṣa me bhartā pārvatyām vihitaḥ purā iti ūṣā vacanam śrutvā sakhī provāca tām tadā tvayā svapne ca yaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ devi tam katham aham samānayiṣyāmi na vijñātaḥ tu yaḥ mama daitya kanyā tat ukte tu rāga aṃdhā maraṇa utsukā rakṣitā ca tayā sakhyā prathame divaseṃ tatas punar provāca sā uṣām vai citralekhā mahāmatiḥ kuṃbhāṃḍasya sutāḥ bāṇa tanayām muni sattama iti uktvā vastra puṭake devān daityān ca dānavān gandharva siddha nāgān ca yakṣa ādīn ca tathā alikhat āsīdavā sakhi aśi śkīti vibhāvaḥ ārṣaḥ iḍā ārṣaḥ ūṣā provāca cauraḥ asau mayā prāptaḥ tu yaḥ niśi puruṣaḥ sakhi yena āśu cetaḥ ranam hṛtam mama tam aham jñātum icchāmi vada sarvam ca bhāmini iti uktā sā abravīt nām yoginī tasya ca anvayam utsukā bāṇa tanyā babhāṣe sā tu kāminī ūṣā uvāca upāyam racaya prītyā tad prāptyai sakhim tad kṣaṇāt yena upāyena tam kāntam labheya prāṇa vallabham yam vinā aham kṣaṇam nā ekam sakhi jīvitum utsahe iti uktvā sā tayā bāṇa ātmajayā maṃtri kanyakā vismitā abhūt muni śreṣṭha su vicāra parā abhavat tatas sakhīm samābhāṣya citralekhā manojavā buddhā tam kṛṣṇa pautram sā dvārakām gaṃtum udyatā jyeṣṭha kṛṣṇa caturdaśyām tṛtīye tu gate ahani ā prabhātāt muhūrte tu saṃprāptāḥ dvārakām purīm ekena kṣaṇa mātreṇa nabhasām divya yaḥ ginī tatas ca aṃtaḥpura udyāne prādyumniḥ dadṛśe tayā krīḍan nārī janaiḥ sārddham prapiban mādhavī madhu sarva aṃga sundara śyāmaḥ su smitaḥ nava yauvanaḥ tatas khām samārūḍham aṃdhakāra paṭena sā tatas sā mūrdhni tām khayam gṛhītvā nimiṣa aṃtarāt saṃprāptā yatra sā bāṇanaṃdinī kāma ārtā vividhān bhāvān cakāra unmatta mānasā ānītam atha tam dṛṣṭvā tadā bhītā ca sā abhavat yāvat krīḍitum ārabdham tāvat jñātam ca tad kṣaṇāt iṃgitaiḥ anumā naiḥ ca kanyā dauḥśīlyam ācaran taruṇaḥ darśanīyaḥ tu sāhasī samara priyaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā sarva mā ācakhyuḥ bāṇāya vali sūnave puruṣāḥ te mahā vīrāḥ kanyā antaḥpura rakṣakāḥ dvārapālāḥ ūcuḥ deva kaścid na jānīte guptaḥ catiḥpure balāt śakraḥ tu tava kanyām vai svayaṃgrāhāt adharṣayat vismitaḥ abhūt muni śreṣṭha kanyāyāḥ śruta dūṣaṇaḥ iti śrī śiva mahā purāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām paṃcame yuddha khaṇḍe ūṣācaritravarṇanam nāma dvipañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā vismitam vākyam kim kāraṇam atha abravīt bāṇaḥ krodha parītā hasan iva aho manuṣyaḥ rūpa āḍhayaḥ sāhasī dhairyavān iti duṣṭa bhāgyaḥ vimūḍha dhīḥ tam mārayadhvam kupitāḥ śīghram śastraiḥ su dāruṇaiḥ durācāram ca tam baddhā ghore kārā gṛhe tatas rakṣadhvam vikaṭe vīrāḥ bahukāḥ laṃ viśeṣataḥ vadhāya tasya vīrasya vyādiṣṭam pāpa buddhinā tad ādiṣṭāḥ tu te vīrāḥ puram drutam chādayāmāsuḥ ati ugrāḥ chiāgha bhiṃdi iti vādinaḥ śatru sainyam tatas dṛṣṭvā garjamānaḥ sa yādavaḥ niṣkrāṃtaḥ bhavanāt tasmāt vajra hastaḥ iva aṃtakaḥ tena tān kikarān hatvā punar catas puram yayau jaghāna roṣa rakta akṣaḥ varddhitaḥ śiva tejasā lakṣe hate atha yodhānām tatas bāṇa asuraḥ ruṣā kuṃbhāṃḍam sa gṛhītvā tu yuddhe śauṃḍam samāhvayat tad vadhāya tatas śaktim kāla vaiśvānara upamām aniruddhaḥ gṛhītvā tām tayā tam nijaghāna hi rathopasthe tatas bāṇaḥ tena śaktyā hataḥ dṛḍham adṛśyamānaḥ tu tadā kūṭa yodhaḥ sa dānavaḥ nānā śastra sahasraiḥ tam jaghāna hi punar punar chadmanā nāgapāśaiḥ tam babaṃdha sa mahā balaḥ bali putraḥ mahā vīraḥ śiva bhakta śśara asuraḥ tam badhvā paṃjara aṃtastham kṛtvā yuddhāt upāramat uvāca bāṇaḥ saṃkruddhaḥ sūtaputram mahā balam bāṇāsuraḥ uvāca yena me dūṣitam pūtam balāt duṣṭena sat phalam chitvā tu sarva gātrāṇi rākṣasebhyaḥ prayaccha bhoḥ atha asya rakta māṃsāni kravyādāḥ api bhuṃjatām agādhe tṛṇa saṃkīrṇe kūpe pātakinam jahi kim bahu uktyā sūtaputra māraṇīyaḥ hi kuṃbhāṃḍaḥ tu atravīt vākyam bāṇam san maṃtri sattamam kuṃbhāṃḍaḥ uvāca ayam tu dṛśyate deva tulyaḥ viṣṇoḥ parākramaiḥ imām avasthām prāptaḥ asi pauruṣe saṃvyavasthitaḥ ayam śiva prasādāt vai kṛṣṇa pautraḥ mahā balaḥ asmān tṛṇa upamān vetti daṣṭaḥ api bhujagaiḥ valāt etat vākyam tu vāṇāya kathayitvā sa dānavaḥ kuṃbhāṃḍaḥ uvāca kena vā tvam iha ānītaḥ durācāra nara adhama daitya indram stuhi vīram tvam namaskuru kṛtāṃjaliḥ jitaḥ asmi iti vacaḥ dīnam kathayitvā punar punar evam kṛte tu mokṣaḥ syāt anyathā baṃdhana ādi niśācara durācāra śatru dharme na vetsi bhoḥ dainyam palāyanam ca atha śūrasya maraṇa adhikam kṣatriyasya raṇe śreyaḥ maraṇam sat mukhe sadā na vīra māninaḥ bhūmau dīnasya iva kṛtāṃjaliḥ ityādi vīra vākyāni bahūni sa jagāda tam tat ākarṇya sa bāṇaḥ sām vismitaḥ abhūcukopa ca tadā uvāca nabhaḥ vāṇī bāṇasya āśvāsanāya hi śṛṇvatām sarva vīrāṇām aniruddhasya maṃtriṇaḥ vyomavāṇī uvāca śivaḥ sarva īśvaraḥ sākṣī karmaṇām parama īśvaraḥ saḥ eva kartā bhartā ca saṃhartā jagatām sadā sarvasya aṃtargataḥ svāmī prerakaḥ sarvatas paraḥ nirvikāri avyayaḥ nityaḥ māyā adhīśaḥ api nirguṇaḥ nāśayiṣyati te garvam idānīm bhakta vatsalaḥ iti ābhāṣya nabhaḥ vāṇī virarāma mahā mune bāṇā suraḥ tad vacanāt aniruddham na jaghnivān mad vākyam ca visasmāra vijahāra viruddha dhīḥ priyayā atṛpta cetāḥ tu durgām sasmāra tad kṣaṇāt āgaccha me kuru trāṇam yaśode caṃḍa roṣiṇī tvām vinā rakṣakaḥ nā anyaḥ stasmāt rakṣa śive hi mām tena ittham toṣitā tatra kālī bhinna aṃjana prabhā jyeṣṭha kṛṣṇa caturdaśyām saṃprāptā āsīt mahā niśi gurubhiḥ muṣṭi nighataiḥ dārayāmāsa paṃjaram śarān tān bhasmasātkṛtvā sarpa rūpān bhayānakān mocayitvā aniruddham tu tatas ca aṃtaḥpuram tatas praveśayitvā durgā tu tatra eva adarśanam gatā ittham devyāḥ prasādāt tu śiva śakteḥ muni īśvara prādyumniḥ vāṇa tanayām priṃyoṃ prāpya sumoda ca pūrvavat vijahām rāsaum tayā sva priyayā sukhī pīta pānaḥ su rakta akṣaḥ sa bāṇa sutayā tatas iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe ūṣācaritre aniruddhoṣāvihāravarṇanam nāma tripaṃcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca tayā kṛṣṇaḥ kim akārṣīt hi tat vada śrutvā ca vyathitaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ babhūva muni sattama kṛṣṇaḥ tad vṛttam akhilam śrutvā yuddham na ca ādarāt jagāma śoṇitapuram tārkṣyamā hūya tad kṣaṇāt kṛṣṇa ādikānām taiḥ tatra rudra ādyaiḥ bāṇa rakṣakaiḥ nandinā garuḍasya api pareṣām ca paraiḥ api yuddham babhūva vipra indra teṣām ca yadu vaṃśi nām bhratrā rāmeṇa sahitaḥ pradyumnena ca dhīmatā samaram atulam pramathaiḥ saha pramathaiḥ vividha ākāraiḥ teṣām atyaṃta dāruṇam yuddham babhūva vikaṭam vṛṣṇīnām roma harṣaṇam vibhīṣikābhiḥ vahvībhiḥ koṭarībhiḥ pade pade nirlajjābhiḥ ca nārībhiḥ prabalābhiḥ adūrataḥ cakruḥ yuddham mahā ghoram śatru sainyam vināśayan tat śrutvā śaṃkara gaṇāḥ vineduḥ yuyudhuḥ ca te mardayan pratiyoddhāram varddhitāḥ śaṃbhu tejasā kṛṣṇa sainyam vidudrāva prativīreṇa nirjitam vidrāvite sva sainye tu śrī kṛṣṇaḥ ca param tapaḥ svam jvaram śītala ākhyam hi vyasṛjat vāruṇam mune vidrāvite kṛṣṇa sainye kṛṣṇasya śītalajvaraḥ maheśvaraḥ atha tam dṛṣṭvā ayāntam svam visṛjat jvaram vaiṣṇavaḥ atha samākraṃdat māheśvara bala arditaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā caritam kṛṣṇaḥ visismāya bhaya anvitaḥ jaghāna śaktyā pradyumnam daitya saṃgha ati amarṣaṇaḥ kuṃbha aṃḍa kūpa karṇābhyām nānā astraiḥ ca samāhataḥ kṛtvā sahasram kāyānām pītvā toyam mahā arṇavāt garuḍaḥ nāśayati atha megha arṇava aṃbuṣu vegena mahatā aram vai śṛṃgābhyām nijaghāna tam śṛṃga ghāta viśīrṇa āgaḥ garuḍaḥ atīva vismitaḥ vidudrāva vihāya ca janārdanam uvāca sārathim śīghram rudra teja uti vismitaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca he sūta śṛṇumat vākyam ratham me vāhaya drutama mahādeva samīpa sthaḥ yathā syām gaditum vacaḥ iti uktaḥ hariṇā sūtaḥ dārukaḥ sva guṇa agraṇīḥ drutam tam vāhayāmāsa ratham rudra samīpataḥ atha vijñāpayāmāsa nataḥ bhūtvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca viśva utpatti sthāna nāśa hetum sat jña ti mātrakam brahma liṃgam param śatim kevalam parameśvaram kṣetram ca prāṇaḥ ātmā ca vikāraḥ tad samūhakaḥ līlayā eva svīkṛtaiḥ nirjara ādikān lokaśas nūnam vibharṣi haṃsi unmārgān svabhāvataḥ tvam eva ca ādyaḥ puruṣaḥ advitīyaḥ taryaḥ ātma dṛś īśaḥ hetuḥ ahetuḥ ca sa vikāraḥ pratīyase yathā eva sūryo pihitaḥ chāyā rūpāṇi ca prabho su acchā yayā saṃcakāsti hi ayam parama dṛś bhavān guṇena apihitaḥ api tvam guṇena eva guṇān vibho sva pradīpaḥ cakāsti tvam bhūman giriśa śaṃkara yaḥ tvad pādau sa śocyaḥ hi ātma vaṃcakaḥ tvad ājñayā aham bhagavān bāṇa dos chettum āgataḥ tu athā eva śaptaḥ bāṇaḥ ayam garvitaḥ garva hāriṇā ājñām dehi prabho me tvam bāṇasya bhuja kṛṃtane pratyuvāca prasanna ātmā kṛṣṇa stutyā maheśvaraḥ satyam uktam tvayā tāta mayā śaptaḥ hi daitya rāj mad ājñayā bhavān prāptaḥ bāṇa dos daṃḍa kṛtane kim karomi ramānātha bhakta adhīnaḥ sadā hare paśyataḥ me katham vīra syāt vāṇa bhuja kṛṃtanam atas tvam jṛṃbhaṇa astreṇa mām jṛṃbhaya mad ājñayā tatas tvam kuru kārya svam yatheṣṭam ca sukhī bhava iti uktaḥ śaṃkareṇa atha śārṅgapāṇiḥ su vismitaḥ svaraṇa sthānam āgatya mumoda sa muni īśvaraḥ jṛṃbhaṇa astram mumoca atha saṃdhāya dhanuṣi drutam pinākapāṇaye vyāsa nānā astra kuśalaḥ hariḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām paṃcame yuddha khaṃḍe bāṇa amurarudrakṛṣṇādiyuddhavarṇanam nāma catuḥ paṃcāśattamaḥ a dhyāyaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca adbhutā iyam kathā tāta śrāvitā me tvayā sune jṛṃbhite jṛṃbhaṇa astreṇa hariṇā samare hare hate vāṇa bale bāṇaḥ kim akārṣīt ca tat vada sūtaḥ uvāca śṛṇu vyāsa mahā prājña kathām ca parama adbhutām kṛṣṇa śaṃkarayoḥ tāta loka līlā anusāriṇoḥ vāṇaḥ vinirgataḥ yuddham kartum kṛṣṇena daitya rāj kuṃbha aṃḍa saṃgṛhītāḥ vaḥ tatas dṛṣṭvā nija balam naṣṭam sa daitya indraḥ ati amarṣitaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ api mahā vīraḥ giriśa āpta mahā balaḥ uccais jagarja tatra ājī vāṇam mattvā tṛṇa upamam dhanuḥ ṭaṃkārayāmāsa śārgāṃkhyam nijam adbhutam trāṃsa kakud doṣaṇī iti bhāṣya udāharaṇāt dās śabdasya napuṃsaka tvam yat bāṇa sainyam tat avaśiṣṭam muni īśvara tena nādena mahatā dhanuḥ ṭaṃkāra jena hi dyāvābhūmyoḥ aṃtaram vai vyāptam āsīt anaṃtaram cikṣepa vivi dhān bāṇān bāṇāya kupitaḥ hariḥ karṇa antam tat vikṛṣya atha tīkṣṇān āśīviṣa upamān āyātaḥ tān nirīkṣya atha sam bāṇaḥ bali nandanaḥ tatra sum vṛṣṇayaḥ sarvam kṛṣṇa ātmānaḥ vicetasaḥ smṛtvā śiva pada ambhojam cikṣepa nija sāyakān sa kṛṣṇāya ati śūrāya mahāgava baleḥ sutaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ api tān asaṃprāptān acchinat sva śaraiḥ drutam smṛtvā śiva pada ambhojam amarāriḥ mahā balaḥ rāma ādayaḥ vṛṣṇayaḥ ca sva svam yoddhāram āhave bāṇa asura balam pakṣa āghātaiḥ amardayat marditam sva balam dṛṣṭvā mardayaṃtam ca tam balī cukopa ati baleḥ putraḥ śaiva rāj ditija īśvaraḥ cikṣepa yugapad vāṇān amitān tatra vīra hā garuḍe ca pṛthak pṛthak jaghāna ekena garuḍam kṛṣṇam ekeṇa patriṇā tatas kṛṣṇaḥ viṣṇu rūpaḥ surāri hā jaghāna bāṇam tarasā śārṅga nissṛta sat śaraiḥ ciccheda tat dhanuḥ śīghram chatra ādi kamanā ākulaḥ hayāḥ ca pātayāmāsa hatvā tān sva śaraiḥ hariḥ kṛṣṇam jaghāna gadayā saḥ apatat dharaṇī tale utthāya āram tatas kṛṣṇaḥ yuyudhe tena śatruṇā śiva bhaktena deva ṛṣe loka līlā anusārataḥ evam dvayoḥ ciram kālam babhūva su mahān raṇaḥ śiva rūpaḥ hariḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sa ca śaiva uttamaḥ balī kṛṣṇaḥ atha kṛtvā samaram ciram bāṇena vīryavān śiva ājñayā muni īśvaraḥ tatas sudarśanena āśu kṛṣṇaḥ bāṇa bhujān bahūn ciccheda bhagavān śaṃbhu śāsanāt para vīra hā avaśiṣṭāḥ bhujāḥ tasya catvāraḥ atīva sundarāḥ gata vyathaḥ babhūva āśu śaṃkarasya prasādataḥ gata smṛtiḥ yadā bāṇaḥ śira chet tam samudyataḥ kṛṣṇaḥ vīra tvam āpannaḥ tadā rudraḥ samutthitaḥ bhagavan devakīputra yat ājñaptam mayā purā tat kṛtam ca tvayā vipra mad ājñā kāriṇā sadā māḥ bāṇasya śiraḥ chidhi saṃhara sva su darśanam dadhīce rāvaṇe vīre tārakā ādi pureṣu api vinā mad ājñām lakṣmīśa rathāṅgam na amucaḥ purā tvam tu yogi īśvaraḥ sākṣāt paramātmā janārdana bhujān kaṇḍūyamānaḥ tu vismṛta ātma garti hare tadā aham aśapam tam vai bhuja chettā āgamiṣyati mad ājñayā hariḥ prāptaḥ bhuja chettā tava atha vai iti uktaḥ sa tayoḥ maitrīm kārayitvā maheśvaraḥ tad anujñāpyaḥ sa gaṇaḥ sva putram sva ālayam yayau iti ākarṇya vacaḥ śaṃbhoḥ saṃhṛtya ca sudarśanam akṣata aṃgaḥ tu vijayī tat kṛṣṇā uṃtaḥpuram yayau aniruddham samāśvāsya sahitam bhāryayā punar jagrāha ratna saṃghātam bāṇadattam anekaśas tad sakhīm citralekhām ca gṛhītvā para yoginīm prasannaḥ abhūt tatas kṛṣṇaḥ kṛta kāryaḥ śiva ājñayā hṛdā praṇamya giriśam āmaṃtrya ca baleḥ sutam dvārakām ca purīm prāptaḥ samutsavam amanvitaḥ visarjayitvā garuḍam sakhīn vīkṣya upahasya ca dvārakāyām tatas dṛṣṭvā kāma cārī cacāra ha iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām paṃcame yuddha khaṃḍe bāṇabhujakṛta nagarvāpahāravarṇanam nāma pañcapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ nāradaḥ uvāca kṛṣṇe gate dvārakāyām aniruddhena bhāryayā tatas bāṇaḥ tattvam vada mahā mune kṛṣṇe gate dvārakāyāḥ maniruddhena bhāryayā duḥkhitaḥ abhūt tatas bāṇaḥ sva ajñānam saṃsmaran hṛdā tatas nandī bāṇam provāca duḥkhitam daityam śoṇita digdha aṃga manu tāpa samanvitam nandīśvaraḥ uvāca bāṇa śaṃkara sat bhakta mā anutāpam kuruṣva bhoḥ bhakta anukaṃpī śaṃbhuvai bhakta vatsala nāmadhṛk manyasva bhakta śārdūla śivam smara punar punar bhakta anukaṃpanaḥ ca asya śaṃkarasya punar punar nandi vākyāt tatas bāṇaḥ dviṣā śīrṣaka mātrakaḥ śiva sthānam jagāma āśu dhṛtvā dhairye mahāmanāḥ gatvā tatra prabhum natvā ruroda atīta vihvalaḥ gata garva trajaḥ bāṇaḥ premā kulita mānasaḥ nana tāṃḍavam mukhyam pratyālīḍha ādi śobhitam mukha vāda sahasrāṇi bhrū kṣepa sahitāni api śiraḥ kampa sahasrāṇi prāpta anīkaḥ sahasraśas vārīḥ ca vividha ākārāḥ darśayitvā śanais śanais tathā śoṇita dhārābhiḥ siñcayitvā mahī talam rudra prasādayāmāsa śūlinam candraśekharam bāṇa asuraḥ mahā bhaktaḥ vismṛta ātma gatiḥ mamaḥ uvāca bāgam saṃhṛṣṭaḥ vāṇa tāta baleḥ putra saṃtuṣṭaḥ nartanena te bāṇena saṃvṛṇītaḥ abhūi~raiḥ tu vraṇa ropaṇe uṣā putrasya rājyam tu tasmin śoṇitaka āhvaye nirvaira tā ca vibudhaiḥ viṣṇunā ca viśeṣataḥ na punar aidaitya tā duṣṭā rajasā tamasā yutā śaṃbhu bhaktiḥ viśeṣeṇa nirvikārā sadā mune śiva bhakteṣu ca snehaḥ dayā sarveṣu jaṃtuṣu iti kṛtvā varān śaṃbho bali putraḥ mahā suraḥ premṇā anayanaḥ rudram tuṣṭāva su kṛta aṃjaliḥ bāṇaḥ uvāca tvām namāmi mahā īśāna dīna bandho dayā nidhe kṛtā mayi kṛpā atīva kṛpā sāgara śaṃkara brahmāṃḍa tanuḥ ugra īśaḥ virāj sarva anvitaḥ paraḥ te manaśū andraḥ tava prabho jaṭharam vārddharbhujā iṃdraḥ dhiṣaṇā vidhiḥ romāṇi auṣadhayaḥ nātha keśāḥ jala mucaḥ tava guṇāḥ trayaḥ tri netrāṇi sarva ātmā puruṣaḥ bhavān brāhmaṇam te mukham prāhuḥ bāhum kṣatriyam eva ca vaiśyam āhuḥ te pāda jam śūdram eva ca tatas śaṃbhoḥ prasādena mahākāla tvam āgataḥ iti kila śara nāmnā śaṃkarasya api vṛttam sakala guru janānām sam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhi tāyām paṃ yuddhakhaṃḍe bāṇāsuragaṇapatvaprāptivarṇanam nāma ṣaṭpaṃcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śṛṇu vyāsa mahā premṇā caritam śaśimau linaḥ yathā avadhī triśūlena dānava indram gaja asuram tasya putraḥ mahā vīraḥ muni īśvara gaja asuraḥ pituḥ vadham hi saṃsmṛtya kṛtam devyā sura arthanāt saṃvicārya eti manasā abhūt tapaḥ rata mānasaḥ jaṭā bhāraiḥ sa vai reje pralaya arkaḥ iva aṃśubhiḥ tena taptāḥ surāḥ sarve divam tyaktvā sa vāsavāḥ brahma lokam yayuḥ vijñāpayāmā su śvacāla bhūḥ devāḥ ūcuḥ na śaknumaḥ divi sthātum atas te śaraṇam gatāḥ vidhehi upa śamam tasya ca anyān jīvayitum kṛpā lokāḥ naṃkṣyati anyathā hi satyam satyam bruvāmahe rā gadgadayā prītaḥ agṛṇāt devam sa māhiṣiḥ gajāsuraḥ uvāca sarveṣām lokapālānām nikhila ddhi su bhuj vibho evam vṛtaḥ śa ta dhṛtiḥ dānavena sa tena vai evam labdha varaḥ daityaḥ māhiṣiḥ ca gaja asuraḥ ityādīn nikhilān jitvā vaśa mānī viśvajit kāle vyatīte pāpa buddhiḥ abhūt tatas mahiṣa asura putraḥ asau saṃcikkeśa dvijān varān tāpasān nitarām pṛthvyām dānavaḥ sura mardanaḥ surān narān ca pramathān sarvān cikkeśa durmatiḥ agaccha drājadhānī vai śaṃkarasya gajāmuraḥ trāta trāta iti tatra āsīt ānaṃda vana vāsinām mahiṣā asa śivasya śaraṇam jagmuḥ natvā tuṣṭuvuḥ ādarāt nyavedayan dānavasya tasya kāśyām samāgamam keśa ādhikyam tatratyānām tad nāthānām viśeṣataḥ deṣā ūcuḥ kaṣṭam datte tvad janānām tam jahi tvam kṛpā nidhe acalām sa calām tatra karoti nija bhārataḥ rakṣasvam bhaktān deveśa kāśī rakṣaṇa tatparaḥ tatra ājagāma soram tad badha kāmanayā haraḥ āgatam tam samālokya śaṃkaṭa bhakta vatsalam triśūla hastam garjatam jagarja sa gaja asuraḥ tatas tayoḥ mahān āsīt samaraḥ dāruṇaḥ adbhutaḥ nānā astra śastra saṃpātaiḥ vīra ārāvam prakurvatoḥ vivyādha giriśam bāṇaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ dānava ghātinam tat śarān cicchide pūrṇam aprāptaḥ tilaśas mune tatas triśūla hetiḥ tam āyāntam daitya puṃgavam gajāsuraḥ uvāca jāne tvām tridiva īśāna triśūlin smara hāriṇam aṃdhakāre maheśāna tripurāṃtaka sarvaga kiṃcid vijñaptum icchāmi tat śṛṇuṣva kṛpā kara vikatthate iti śeṣaḥ jagatām vaṃdyaḥ viśvasya upari saṃsthitaḥ prahasya pratyuvāca īśaḥ māhiṣeyam gaja asuram īśvaraḥ uvāca gaja asura prasannaḥ asmi sva anukūlam varam vṛṇu pratyuvāca prasanna ātmā dāna vā indraḥ gaja asuraḥ gajāsuraḥ uvāca imām kṛttim maheśāna tva triśūla anipāvitām darśanīyām mahā divyām sarvadā eva sukha āvahām sadā eva nirmalā ca astu sadā eva astu ati maṃḍanām na dagdhā kṛttiḥ eṣā me puṇya gaṃdha nidheḥ tatas yadi puṇyavatī nā eṣā mama kṛtti diś aṃbara tathā iti uvācam su prītaḥ maṃhiṣa asura jam ca tam punar provāca prīta ātmā dānavam tam gaja asuram īśvaraḥ uvāca harṣam āpuḥ janāḥ sarve kāśī sthāḥ pramathāḥ tathā tuṣṭuvuḥ tam maheśānam natvā sajila yaḥ tatas iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe gajāsuravadhaḥ nāma saptapaṃcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ yathā duṃdubhi nirhrādam avadhīt itijam haraḥ viṣṇu devena kālena prāpa duḥkham mahat ditiḥ sāṃtvayāmāsa tām vāgbhiḥ duḥkhitām nā ārṣaḥ kha daḥ atha daityaḥ sa māyāvī ditim āśvāsya daitya rāj devaiḥ ca ghātitaḥ vīraḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ mahā asuraḥ viṣṇunā ca saha bhrātrām sa chalaiḥ daitya vairibhiḥ yatas kratu bhujaḥ devāḥ kratavaḥ veda saṃbhavāḥ iti duṃdubhi nirhrādaḥ mārtam kṛtvā kula ucitām niśśaṃkam bhakṣayati evam na tyajati api kīkaśam jagat rakṣāḥ bhaveyuḥ iti śeṣaḥ rudra māyā antam ālokyaḥ tad bhakta ācita liṅgataḥ ati ārta māra dvaya āmraḥ rodasī pūrayan mṛtaḥ tena nādena mahatā saṃpravepita mānasāḥ tuṣṭavuḥ praṇatāḥ ssarve śarve jaya jaya akṣaraiḥ brāhmaṇāḥ ūcuḥ duṣṭān gaurīśa bhaktebhyaḥ dehi ca abhayam saṃgrāme praviśan mati om jayam āpnoti asaṃśayam etasmin aṃtare devāḥ samājagmuḥ sa vāsavāḥ praṇamya śaṃkaram premṇā sarve sāṃjalayaḥ surāḥ devāḥ ūcuḥ etad dudubhi nirhrāda vadhā trātāḥ vayam surāḥ vadhyāḥ khala aśva deveśa tvayā sarva īśvara prabho te api viprāḥ mahā harṣāt punar yātāḥ yathāgatam idam ākhyānam atulam śiva līlā amṛta akṣaram param bhakti pradam dhanyam śiva prīti karam śivam parama jñāna dam ramyam vikāra haraṇam param iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvi rudrasaṃhitāyām pañca yuddha khaṇḍe duṃdubhinirhrādadaityavadhavarṇanam nāma aṣṭapaṃcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śṛṇu vyāsa su saṃprītyā caritam parimeśituḥ yathā avadhīt sva priyayā daityam uddiśya sam jñayā āstām purā mahā daityau vidala utpala saṃjñakau lāt tābhyām sarve surāḥ brahman daityābhyām nirjitāḥ raṇe tābhyām parājitāḥ devāḥ vidheḥ te śaraṇam gatāḥ natvā tam vidhivat sarvam kathayāmāsuḥ āda rāt dhairyam kuruta saṃsmṛtya sa śivam śivam ādarāt bhakta vatsala nāmā asau sa śivaḥ śam karaḥ śivaḥ iti uktvā tān tatas brahmā tūṣṇīm āsīt śivam smaran te api devāḥ mudam prāpya sva svam dhāma yayuḥ tadā atha nārada devarṣiḥ śiva preraṇayā tadā gatvā tadīya bhavanam śivā sauṃdaryam ājagau śrutvā tad vacanam daityāḥ vā āstām māyā vimohitau vicārayāmāsatuḥ tau kadā kutra śivā ca sā bhaviṣyati vidheḥ prāpta udayā nnau iti sarvadā sphuṭat cola aṃśuka patha tiryad aṃga prabhā vṛtā ullasat kaṃduka āsphāla ati śroṇita kara ambujā atha duṣṭa nihaṃtrā vai sa avajñena hareṇa tau vijñātau ca kṣaṇāt āstām daityau imau iti gaṇau na iti sarva svarūpiṇā atha sā netra saṃjñām sva svāḥ minaḥ tām bubodha ha mahā kautukinaḥ tāta śaṃkarasya pareśituḥ mahā balau mahādevyā kaṃṭukena samāhatau kaṃdukeśvara saṃjñām ca tat liṃgam abhavat tadā śiva āvirbhāvam ājñāya vṛṣayaḥ ca samāyayuḥ atha sarve surāḥ śambhoḥ varān prāpya tad ājñayā sva dhāmāni yayuḥ prītāḥ tathā kāśī nivāsinaḥ sa aṃbikam śaṃkaram dṛṣṭvā kṛtām jali puṭāḥ ca te praṇamya tuṣṭuvuḥ bhaktyā vāgbhiḥ iṣṭābhiḥ ādarāt sa vikaḥ api śivaḥ vyāsa krīḍitvā su vihāra vid jagāma sva ālayam prītaḥ sa gaṇaḥ bhakta vatsalaḥ kaṃdukeśvara liṃgam ca kāśyām duṣṭa nivarhaṇam idam ākhyānam atulam śṛṇuyāt yaḥ mudā anvitaḥ śivayoḥ bhakta vātsalya sūcakam śiva dam satām iti uktvā āmaṃtrya tam vyāsam tad nutaḥ mad vara ātmajaḥ yayau vihāyasā kāśīm caritam śaśimaulinaḥ iyam hi saṃhitā raudrī sampūrṇā varṇitā mayā imām yaḥ ca paṭhet nityam śatru bādhā nivārikām sūtaḥ uvāca iti brahmasuta śrutvā pitrā śiva yaśaḥ param śata nāmā āpya śaṃbhoḥ ca kṛtārthaḥ abhūt śiva anugaḥ brahma nārada samvādaḥ sampūrṇaḥ kathitaḥ mayā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcame yuddha khaṃḍe vidalotpaladaityavadhavarṇanam nāma ekonaṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ yaḥ yuddha khaṃḍaḥ samāptā iyam dvitīyām rudra saṃhitā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe dvitīyāyām rudrasaṃhitāyām pañcamaḥ yuddha khaṇḍaḥ samāptaḥ samāptā iyam dvitīyā rudra saṃhitā
